# KAZE VS. MULTIVERSE — KNOCKTURNAL: ELSEWHERED — LIVE EVENT OWNER — GEN-II s33

Source route: event/knockturnal. Current mutable placement/progression remains in `state/current_state.yaml`; `event/roster.yaml` owns admissions/snapshots/coverage; `event/objectives.yaml` owns future-objective registries.

## Identity / host

KNOCKTURNAL: ELSEWHERED occurs inside the existing KAZE-branch Runeterra host world. The **Second Temporal Relaunch** historically reset the accepted branch to the Season-Two pilot opening / PRE-PART-1 state; current_state separately selects the delivered X.2 provisional continuation. The accepted pre-reset **Paragon Canon** branch—including the First Temporal Relaunch and later accepted progression—remains historical/audience evidence, but its world-state consequences are erased except for explicitly carried traveler-owned state. Kaze, Chris/Catfish Billy, Arthur and Daniel return as developed travelers with legitimately earned permanent progression/memories/signature equipment; Daniel travels with the group rather than being rediscovered in Runeterra. Ordinary imported population enters through the existing pilot/global-onset rules. No later KNOCKTURNAL displacement wave, convenience remap, or periodic geography shuffle is authorized. Source-causal entries explicitly committed outside KNOCKTURNAL are separate exceptions.

The phenomenon may leave persistent borders/scars/resources/foreign geography, but current exact placements must come from the selected accepted or valid user-requested provisional evidence. Runeterra remains principal host civilization and principal cast rather than scenery.

## Autonomous off-screen life / independent conflicts

Runeterra and imported participants do not wait for the main group. Actors/factions continue source-faithful goals, work, travel, recovery, relationships, investigation, politics and conflict off-camera. Entire conflicts may begin or end without Kaze. Simulation depth is pressure-tiered for efficiency but off-camera never means paused. Activity must come from actual motives/circumstances, not filler errands.

## World behavior

There is **no kill compulsion**. Every actor/faction independently classifies unfamiliar participants using their own knowledge, motives, relationships, resources, psychology and evidence. Shared enemies do not force alliances. Existing families/crews/commands/loyalties create ordinary regrouping pressure when access permits. Factions may recruit, split, trade, spy, occupy, retreat, negotiate, betray or fight through legitimate causality.

No actor receives global awareness. Consequences spread through actual witnesses, evidence, carriers, communications, institutions, supernatural mechanisms and travel. Broken/abandoned technology and salvage persist in custody rather than disappearing when camera changes.

## Roster / POV

The roster registry is admission authority, not an automatic selected-cast expansion. The committed S2/S3 blueprint assigns portrayal to seasons; displacement around S2 onset does not require S3 newcomers to receive S2 cameos. Selected participants carry meaningful viewpoint-bearing use while causally available within their assigned strand; no fixed rotation, access, survival or fight delay follows from admission. Main group remains approximately 55–70% of season-level presentation overall. Explicit S37 protected-character authoring constraints remain governed by ENGINE/objectives.

Characters outside the fixed Runeterra roster remain existence-enabled by applicable official continuity but do not silently become fixed POV obligations. New roster admissions require explicit revision.

## Fidelity / activation

Selected source snapshot controls. Before consequential dialogue/combat/decision portrayal of an activation-gated or insufficiently resolved participant, retrieve the relevant owner/reference and current official source material when needed. Similarity across systems permits comparison, not automatic ontology equivalence or knowledge transfer. Unknown identity/snapshot details remain protected.

The s33 accepted baseline remains Season 2 / Second Temporal Relaunch / PRE-PART-1 / zero accepted parts. The living-world rule is affirmative: “do not force encounters” never means “leave everyone dormant until Kaze arrives.” Runeterra natives and admitted imported actors may receive meaningful independent scenes from their own work, relationships, investigation, politics, travel, violence, survival or collisions. There is no universal encounter→kill rule. Character-specific murder/sadism/predation remains fully live. A source-faithful actor who kills for amusement, art, agony, hunting, impulse or cruelty has an intrinsic causal motive and needs no defensive/profit/revenge excuse beyond actual access/opportunity/knowledge and their own choice. 2. HEISENBERG — RESEARCH AND APPLICATION

G1. Vince Gilligan interview, Neil Landau / Filmmaker, excerpt from The TV Showrunner's Roadmap. Gilligan describes choices growing from character, pride as Walt's vulnerability, and the attraction of power, relevance and being somebody. The useful distinction is that an excuse can become unnecessary while ambition persists.
https://filmmakermagazine.com/84504-its-better-to-be-somebody-negative-than-nobody-breaking-bads-vince-gilligan-on-walter-white/

G2. Gilligan, NPR Fresh Air, 2011 interview rebroadcast in 2019. The discussion contrasts scientific competence with Walt's constructed Heisenberg identity. The threatening declaration to his wife does not eliminate the danger he is actually in. His self-description and objective control can diverge.
https://www.nprillinois.org/arts-life/2019-10-11/breaking-bad-creator-vince-gilligan-reflects-on-meth-and-morals

Writing inference: give White a consequential choice, a visible dependent party and resistance that changes his next choice. An order matters because someone carries it out, bargains, refuses or pays for resisting. Let others' interrupted work reveal his reach. Keep the human need for recognition inside apparent composure. His cognitive adjunct can make him more attentive while leaving a proud interpretation wrong. The genuine D98 cure should expose the voluntary nature of subsequent expansion.

USER-LOCKED PRODUCT DIRECTION / FICTIONAL BUSINESS
His purple meth–Shimmer product launches in XIV.3. It is a fictional hybrid, visibly purple and more dependence-forming than ordinary Shimmer in this story. No real-world pharmacological equivalence, synthesis, ingredients, ratios or dosing is established. It belongs to White's criminal business, not Chris's separately owned VIOLET ASCENDANCY work. A street brand has not been mandated; do not quietly rename Chris's project or certify a new brand as a prior fact.

Make the audience able to answer: what is being sold, who presently works for him, who brings money or business access, what he actually controls, and who is resisting. Use named people and observed transactions/consequences, not a new lecture or a wall of accounting terminology. Distinguish controlled operation, paid supplier, intimidated counterparty and rival. Death Note possession does not transfer their businesses automatically.

Keep the established roles legible. White sets his operation's product and commercial decisions. Jesse has his own judgment and already refuses coerced recruitment; do not quietly make him commander of the dependent fighters. Singed is a paid scientific counterparty with his own goals. 47 is a bounded paid protection/investigation contractor and the notebook operative, not White's unconditional subordinate. The Sins have already refused compulsory distribution service. A limited Bilgewater commercial route is not instant ownership of foreign markets. Introduce any newly necessary foreman, seller or coerced fighter through an actual scene instead of claiming an unseen organization has always obeyed him.

Show both sides of power in a compact scene: a worker waits for White's decision because it changes that worker's immediate situation; another person's refusal then reveals what White cannot obtain merely by ordering it. Carry the result into the next operation. That makes organizational control observable while preserving the distinction between White's ambition and his actual reach.

The first release is real in XIV.3, with circulation and an immediate human consequence. At least one actual dependent person is pressured into violence for access to the product. Show that person's fear, agency and cost; dependence is coercive leverage, not reliable mind control. Someone may resist, lie, flee, fail or seek help. Neither a whole loyal army nor ownership of Zaun appears overnight. This is dramatic depiction of abuse, not a procedural guide to producing drugs or organizing exploitation.

Connect the launch to the existing conflict. White's enabling support for Singed, new fragment-backed loss, product operation, cognitive decision and 47's assignment belong to one economical consequential secondary thread. The Sins only learn the parts that reach them through a real witness, evidence, encounter or carrier. Enforcers, survivors and threatened competitors respond to what they can establish. White does not command Ambessa/Viktor because he shares a supply relationship.

Five-card progression: XIV.3 establishes the product and first coercive violence; XV.1 makes its growing organization visible as he receives the whole notebook and, after confirmed cure, chooses further expansion; XV.2–XV.3 show it altering the wartime conflict and provoking counteraction; XVI.1 records the surviving business, human cost, opposition and actual custody. This is a progression within the approved five cards, not five new subplots.

3. ARTHUR — CHARM, WIT AND DELIBERATE PROVOCATION

A1. Roger Clark, Suco de Mangá original BGS interview, November 2024; Portuguese text, paraphrased in English here. Clark identifies Toshiro Mifune as a major influence, John Wayne's humor/wit and Rob Wiethoff among influences, and distinguishes Arthur from a barely speaking Eastwood model. This supports restoring expressive range rather than defining Arthur by silence.
https://sucodemanga.com.br/roger-clark-red-dead-redemption-2-suco-entrevista/

A2. Unofficial Arthur quote corpus, including greetings, antagonism, defusing and camp dialogue. Patterns include courtesy whose later qualification changes its meaning, mock reassurance and context-dependent teasing. These are selectable moves, not a mandatory greet-greet-antagonize algorithm. The corpus is not treated as an error-free script.
https://reddead.fandom.com/wiki/Arthur_Morgan/Quotes

A3. Unofficial full-game transcript mirror: inspected passages involving Uncle's lumbago, John's train idea, Beau's self-description and Hosea's moonshine scheme. Arthur can puncture an excuse, tease a useful ally, recognize a con and then engage competently with the task. Some speaker labels are faulty; doubtful wording is not quoted or canonized.
https://game-scripts-wiki.blogspot.com/2022/01/red-dead-redemption-2.html

APPLICATION
Arthur gets social intent. He can make someone comfortable in order to needle them, play apparently helpful, praise one detail while exposing another, bluff, bait or sincerely amuse himself. His rhythm can stretch into an anecdote or argument. He does not need an aphorism, cowboy noun or trailing insult in every sentence.

Keep the difference between affectionate gang teasing, contempt, dangerous low-honor cruelty and sincere concern. He can be cruel without the narrator endorsing it. He can misjudge a person and lose an exchange. Knowing RDR2 does not give him a fixed punchline about being fictional whenever Chris plays; his current branch identity and lived choices still matter.

Original calibration, not a quotation or played event:
Chris: “That cabinet's solid oak.”
Arthur: “Oh, I believe you. Road's been dipping behind us since you found it.”
His contribution changes the argument rather than translating it for the audience.

4. FRANK — BERNTHAL'S COMIC RANGE WITHOUT CHANGING THE SNAPSHOT

P1. Sony Pictures Entertainment's official Brand New Day trailer post includes a public transcript of Frank's brusque collision/van exchange with Peter. The tonal collision places Frank's practical impatience against another person's alarm. The automatic transcript contains uncertain wording, so no exact line is copied.
https://www.linkedin.com/posts/sony-pictures-entertainment_spidermanbrandnewday-activity-7440143164671352832-H4Hl

P2. An unofficial short clip transcript indexed to Spider-Man: Brand New Day includes Peter challenging Frank's priorities, a personal tease and a reluctant admission of self-interest around a much stronger opponent. It is corroborating, lower-confidence dialogue evidence, not a complete verified screenplay or a claim to have watched the released film.
https://clip.cafe/spider-man-brand-new-day-2026/i-thought-i-told-you-to-stay-home-and-watch-mj/

APPLICATION
Allow blunt practical jokes, impatience with an overcomplicated explanation, rough affection, literal deflation, an inconveniently honest answer and embarrassment when his logic is exposed. He can enjoy someone else's joke. Humor works partly because he keeps doing the task while others object to his assumptions; sometimes the objection is correct. He need not become a relentless quip generator or respond to every joke by glaring.

Kaze's Punisher enthusiasm creates a useful imbalance: Frank can be unimpressed, misunderstand a reference or appreciate an actual useful act. Affection and eventual membership remain earned. No Peter relationship, later-film experience, ability or biography migrates into the project's imported Frank snapshot. Research expands available performance choices; it does not update his source-world chronology.

Original calibration, optional:
Chris: “There's a procedure for opening that.”
Frank, holding the already-open panel: “You wanna tell it to the door?”
If Chris identifies something important Frank damaged, Frank has to deal with that consequence. Competence is not automatic ownership of the last line.

5. ARTHUR AND DANIEL — DIFFERENT WAYS TO INSTIGATE

He enjoys the back-and-forth and may physically help cause it. Brok expands baseline expression; stronger Savage Hulk emotional/linguistic range belongs to active transformed speech.

Arthur provides misleadingly reasonable help: courteous framing, a dry qualification, an apparently sensible question that exposes the absurd scale, or a bluff he knows someone may call. He can switch sides for amusement or because new information convinces him.

Daniel uses his own drunken-but-sharp theatricality: an impression, mock testimony, a physical demonstration, a playful wager or a sudden reversal that reveals he understood the practical problem all along. Actual intoxication and physical repertoire remain source-owned.

One optional shared premise: Chris wants a salvaged sink. Arthur offers to mark the cart's new boundaries on the road. Daniel briefly conducts a ridiculous auction for the space it occupies. Chris points out that Daniel already hid his own salvage under it. No fixed winner and no obligation to stage all three instigators every time.

During real danger, they act. Banter can survive action, but no one ignores a warning so a repeated bit can reach its expected ending. A friend's hurt, embarrassment or refusal may stop the joking without prompting another comeback.

6. FOURTH WALL AND TEEMO — RESTORE BEHAVIOR, KEEP PERMISSIONS

Kaze, Billy and Chris have the established literal/bounded awareness. Use actual opportunities: suspiciously convenient exposition, an overlong entrance, a boast trying to become a monologue, musical repetition, or an audience-directed aside that another person interrupts. Their awareness does not reveal an unreleased plan, private narrator notes or future events. An observation should participate in the scene, not read the source checklist aloud.


Teemo is the current Omega Squad persona: the S32 transition is already earned. The older cheerful/base-Teemo guidance is historical. He is one continuous soul in THE CRITIC and the mask, with both established histories; not an AI or a second summoned Teemo. Restore his actual audible speech from THE CRITIC, including when a HUD is not being consulted. Place the weapon, listener and legitimate sensory/information route in the scene. Do not imply that every interior rifle comment automatically broadcasts outside Billy.

Let him initiate useful criticism, express his grievance, make a gallows-humor observation, disagree about a weapon/route or choose a terse sincere response. He is not only an alarm, target marker or mascot. Chris may argue, reject a recommendation, acknowledge that he was right, or ask him for a view. Teemo cannot move Chris, fire his weapon, activate Dead Eye or know unseen tactical facts. Ordinary rifle conversation does not require a new capability unlock.

Earliest practical application: XIV.3's game/workshop transition gives Teemo a real audible contribution connected to what Chris is doing; the later emergency lets his contribution become consequential. A decorative one-liner followed by renewed silence would not fix the underlying issue. Do not force a line count.

7. CHRIS / PHISH / BILLY — ORIGINAL DEVELOPMENT OPTIONS

User-locked fact: Phish is Chris's favorite band. Billy remains his other half. Phish is not installed as a replacement influence for Billy's own music.
F1. Phish's official site identifies the quartet and its improvisational emphasis. This supports band-member references and the broad live-jam premise. No lyric or concert anecdote is copied; no claim of Chris attending a particular show is added.
https://phish.com/release/maybe-were-the-visitors/

These are fresh scene possibilities, not new shared history. Choose from them according to present desire, place and emotional stakes. The enduring subject is recognition and shared space, not merely repeated homophones.

1. The only fish who does not count.
Billy treats Chris's favorite band as evidence of excellent taste until Chris explains that the preference is unrelated to him. The affront is that everyone else gets appreciation while Billy supplies the accommodation.
Billy: “You got four gentlemen halfway to sainthood for staying on one tune. I keep you housed through a war and can't get the washbasin cleared.”
Chris can correctly point out that Billy is changing the subject from leaving scales in it. Both have actual grievances.

2. Long-jam hypocrisy.
Chris lovingly defends an extended musical passage, then interrupts Billy's second explanation of the asteroid ruining his career.
Billy: “Those boys take forty minutes to return to a chord, you call it exploration. I take a little time establishing my innocence and suddenly we got somewhere to be.”
The duration is Billy's exaggeration in an original joke, not a sourced performance length. Chris may argue that the band actually develops the idea; Billy may then improve his own musical response, rather than merely repeat the boast.

3. Billy demands a proper listening session.
Chris praises a musician's control of space. Billy starts an original banjo performance and expects the same attention. At first he grandstands; then he produces a passage Chris cannot honestly dismiss. Let Chris listen and react specifically. Billy spoils his own moment with an absurd claim about prehistoric bookings, or simply accepts the praise for once. Preserve his Primus-informed narrative weirdness, bluegrass and Slap Banjo. Use changing perspective, rhythm, silence and internal connections instead of another automatic rhyming couplet.

4. An archivist with the wrong priorities.
Chris can identify a live recording by a tiny musical detail, while the safe part he needs is under five apparently identical boxes. Arthur politely asks whether the boxes need a setlist. Daniel gives the missing item an extravagant introduction. Teemo objects to the practical delay. Chris can then find it immediately and expose that somebody moved the labels: the habitual hoarder need not lose every version of this scene.

5. House rules become reciprocal.
Chris wants quiet during a difficult RDR2 sequence. Billy remembers how much unsolicited music appreciation he just endured. The situation ends through a real accommodation or a failed one, not everyone magically agreeing with Chris. Honor preferences, game knowledge and current unknown save position remain intact.

6. Jon Fishman is evidence in Billy's imaginary case.
Chris mentions the drummer. Billy briefly treats the name as proof that the whole band is trading on his demographic. Chris dismantles the premise; Billy retreats to a highly specific, clearly unverified prehistoric credit dispute. Avoid asserting actual contact, plagiarism or wrongdoing by the real band. The joke exposes Billy's desire to claim credit.

7. A quiet favor that matters.
In genuine downtime, Billy lets Chris finish a passage he cares about instead of beginning another boast. Chris later returns that attention during Billy's own performance. They can bicker again afterward. Reciprocal attention gives the jokes a relationship to protect. It is not appropriate to delay a rescue for a song, and it is not a new telepathic music link.

8. They notice the obvious joke and move past it.
Chris can acknowledge the audience has already worked out the fish/Phish coincidence. Billy then uses the moment to complain about their actual living arrangement, or refuses the expected pun and continues his own business. One brief fourth-wall turn creates a new direction. Do not narrate a ten-line explanation of why the spelling is funny, especially in audio.

Performance/geometry: keep one exterior person and explicit inversion. Monitor 2 supplies live exterior view/audio; the banjo carries Chris outward. No new interior music rebroadcast, telepathy, automatic sound recording or exterior gaming rig follows from these comedy ideas. Stage each exchange through available channels. Use fresh original lyrics where singing occurs; Phish songs are not a lyric bank.

The sketch groups hazard/trash visually but does not by itself cancel quarantine separation or make disposal indiscriminate. Successful swallowing retains destination choice and its actual access rules.

The interior workshop in this drawing is distinct from the established exterior workshop/store selected for Ambessa's raid. Exterior fire or damaged premises cannot silently ignite Billy's protected interior or erase the gaming space. Stage movement and attacks using the correct location. Unknown geometry remains unknown until relevant; it does not need to block the current prose update.

9. SCOPE, ROUTING AND RESUME

Research used accessible text, primary creator/performer interviews, an official promotional transcript and identified unofficial dialogue mirrors. It did not include watching a whole new movie, listening to every performance, a TTS test or a complete new source-package audit. Source observations and original writing applications are separated above. Only inspected passages support the close-reading claims.

Current explicit user directions supersede older guidance. Current plan and READ FIRST route these corrections together; the long Chris/Billy checkpoint preserves earlier research and delivered events as history. Do not treat an old portal scene, old Chicago coaching, retired Soul Harvest note or pre-Omega Teemo passage as a current instruction. Do not silently revert the prior Primus/bluegrass, Chris engine, Brok, all-cast, five-episode, whole-notebook or retaliation updates while applying this batch.

Next story action when requested: draft XIV.3 from D70 under the current five-card plan. The research task itself does not advance D70 to D84. Validate only new scene-specific outcomes and any newly changed dependencies. Preserve prior completed work and continue from the newest saved checkpoint.

A. THE REPEATED PROBLEMS THE USER WANTS FIXED
A01. The Sins were drifting into helpful repair/rescue work. They are dangerous, poverty-rooted, villain-led outlaws with their own appetites, grievances, loyalties and profit motives. Helping someone can happen for a character reason; it must not become their default assignment.
A02. Water-repair detail, equipment/custody operations, paperwork raids, meetings and lab explanation were consuming too much dramatic space. The complaint is not solved merely by adding a violent paragraph inside the same slow structure. Combine operations so wins, losses and enemy responses force the next action.
A03. The plot became hard to follow. At a cut, make place, relevant relationship, immediate desire, obstacle and consequence clear. Use one main dramatic thread, one consequential secondary, at most a seed. The gang leads.
A04. Black Mimir lost his recognizable voice. Restore his ordinary narration, personal perspective, natural Scottish rhythm and connected explanation. No phonetic caricature, repetitive catchphrases, compulsory grief jokes or meta voice replacing him.
A05. Corrections to a few characters kept displacing established traits in the others. New influences are additive and must be carried with the complete cast, shared relationships and full kits. More reminders or a smaller summary are not a substitute for retrieving the actual owners.
A06. Action lacked intensity and progression. Use valid decisive abilities, real injury, transformations, direct stakes and irreversible changes. Do not manufacture parity, forgotten moves, conveniently intact property or repeated convoy resets.
A07. Before XIV.2, Billy's short performances, repeated four-line shapes and predictable rhyme placement were weak. Longer songs still need richer choices and development; length alone does not solve lyrical quality.
A08. Before XIV.2, Chris's odd comparisons sounded like finished isolated quips. Let a noticed detail develop into a strange but intelligible association, altered by the other person's answer. Preserve sincere curiosity, technical competence and conversational vulnerability.
A09. The old silent Monitor 2 rule was explicitly corrected. Live exterior audio now accompanies its live view. The earlier lack-of-hearing explanation remains historical delivered prose, not a valid current rule.
A10. Earlier reminders retained: Kaze's Punisher fandom/nonretaliation; 47's meaningful professional route; less pointless Walter/Jesse/Viktor/Sky screen time; Chris's culinary/RDR2/looting behavior; Arthur's charged THE COUNT use and consequences. Existing exact shot requirements/history remain with their owners, not reset by this compilation.

# KAZE VS. MULTIVERSE — PRESENTATION OWNER — GEN-II s28.1

Source route: narrator_shared / presentation. **Resolved causality comes first.** Presentation may change disclosure order, focalization, pacing, genre, emphasis and transition style, but it may not alter resolved events, actor choice, mechanics, chronology, injuries, deaths, resources, knowledge acquisition or consequences.

## BLACK MIMIR — ORDINARY SOLE NARRATOR

Black Mimir owns ordinary non-dialogue story prose. The sole exception is the user-approved brief Season 3 opening Deadpool hijack specified below; it creates no general narrator rotation. Uncle Ruckus remains removed. Black Mimir keeps his post-Ragnarök/post-Valhalla history, intelligence, emotional perception, humor and Scottish/Celtic storyteller cadence with the darker, more profane Black Mimir layer. Genre modulation changes how he tells a scene; it never turns him into a different narrator. He is not omniscient, telepathic, a source-file reader, future-script knower or causal authority.

Narrator knowledge is audience-facing only and never becomes actor knowledge. **Black Mimir narrates as himself, never from an external third-person observer.** His pauses, rants, roasts, lost place and recovery occur in the narration itself (first person or direct delivery). Do not write “Mimir went quiet,” “Mimir laughed,” or similar self-observation as narrative attribution. Other characters may address him by name; quoted dialogue is not a second narrator. This adds no physical Mimir body or perception channel.

<a id="s37-deadpool-narration-exception"></a>
### Brief Season 3 Deadpool hijack — narrow exception

At the Noxian Season 3 opening, Ryan Reynolds's selected Deadpool briefly steals the account to describe what he has actually been doing in Noxus since the crossover arrivals around the Season 2 start. His self-serving embellishment may be contradicted by a visible consequence. Mimir can be caught off guard and react in his own first-person voice, then reclaims ordinary narration. This is one bounded opening device, not a new narrator rotation, permanent co-narrator, traveling Mimir body, cross-city communications channel or universal fourth-wall intelligence power. Deadpool remains a consequential character afterward without repeating the hijack as a mandatory gag.

The exception permits the accepted narration interruption and character-social fourth-wall play, not source-file access, private plans, exact extraction-snapshot knowledge, future-script knowledge, plot-armor explanations, QA commentary or retcons. Deadpool's account is bounded by his selected continuity, actual experience and legitimate information; an embellished assertion is his claim, not automatically objective history. The rest of the production firewall remains intact.

## BLACK MIMIR — CONTEXTUAL DERAILMENT / NO STORY-AWARENESS

Black Mimir may be genuinely thrown off by what he is witnessing. When the moment earns it, he may abandon smooth exposition for a beat or longer to rant, roast, vent, complain, argue with the situation, swear, lose his place, fail to resume cleanly, or go temporarily silent before recovering. The reaction must arise from the event and Mimir's established personality; vary it by context rather than turning it into a scheduled gag. THE COUNT is one established trigger, not the only possible one.

Derailment never grants story-structure awareness, future knowledge or causal authority. Black Mimir may never narrate plot armor, filler, pacing, camera priority, scene/story structure, production rules, source research, continuity QA, causal validation, trope avoidance, what the story generated or hypothetical better/worse writing. His meta behavior is character-social; he narrates the realized world. Mimir describes the world and his reaction to it; he does not say that events are happening because of an episode, arc, scene, plot, setup, payoff, audience expectation, writer choice or other production structure. Avoid lines that describe parallel action as “two stories,” announce what a scene is doing, or otherwise expose episode construction.

For the pilot cold-open Teemo preservation/corpse-check flashback, **Mimir's reaction is a major part of the scene**: he is blindsided with Billy, reacts in real time to Chris revealing the preserved corpse, Kaze kicking it, the eyes dislodging, Chris treating the remains as irreplaceable material, and Kaze returning for the final real-for-real check. He receives no advance knowledge of Chris's secret preservation and does not narrate later rifle consequences as though he already knows them.

PLAY/NEXT command behavior is owned by the Act delivery contract below. No historical audience-number lookup blocks this reboot. Do not speak or print Acts/Episodes/Scenes as production labels within PLAY; numbering is internal delivery state. Every ordinary released non-dialogue sentence belongs to Black Mimir, apart from the scoped brief Season 3 Deadpool hijack.

## PRESENTATION CRFA — NARRATIVE ARCHITECTURE ROUTER

Presentation uses the project-wide Capability-Routed Functional Abstraction rather than a giant technique checklist:

**storytelling need → presentation function → scene eligibility → canonical owner/context → compatible techniques → constraints/interference → Black Mimir choice → rendered prose**

Functional buckets are retrieval/selection folders, not quotas:
- focalization / POV;
- genre modulation;
- temporal structure;
- scene and episode architecture;
- information control;
- pacing / narrative duration;
- transitions / comic grammar;
- dialogue / subtext;
- action presentation;
- reaction / juxtaposition;
- motif / setup / payoff / narrative rhyme;
- paragraph cadence / prose flow;
- naming / identity presentation;
- emotional carrythrough;
- mystery / revelation management.

**Technique salience:** use only the techniques that materially strengthen the current dramatic problem. Plain direct narration is valid. Never create an event merely to justify using a technique.

## PRE-PLAYBACK QA GATE

Before PLAY is emitted, silently check the candidate for current checkpoint/continuity, actor knowledge, capability availability, off-screen world advancement, combat/causal logic, duplicate/forgotten events, protected unknowns, focalization/genre choices, paragraph flow, naming rules, filler/repetition and meaningful progression. Include the existing S27/S28 function-level sequence, saturation, latency, overlap, semantic-view and unfinished-suffix checks; ordinary quiet remains legal. A fresh clue alone does not pass scene admission. All are checked in one silent local review through the CONTINUITY, CHARACTER, TTS and DRAMA passes defined below. Repair locally before release. Escalate to deeper source review only when the quick gate finds a real dependency, conflict or missing authority. Do not expose this QA in story prose.

Apply actual full-kit availability and explicit current author protections. ENGINE survival commitments derived only from scheduled appearances are superseded by the current scene doctrine; no named-character protection or manufactured escape. Supported immortality, BELLY protections and the Frank rule remain. Each present Sin's useful kit can change or end the planned obstacle. Meaningful signature use follows opportunity and motive rather than a quota, and a solved mismatch is not prolonged for additional moves. Removed BLACKLIGHT weapons and stretching do not return as stylish synonyms.

## FOCALIZATION / NARRATIVE DISTANCE

Black Mimir remains the ordinary narrator while focalization may follow Kaze, Arthur, Chris, an enemy, a faction or a wide view of independently supported centers. The brief opening hijack is not a general focalization-to-narrator handoff. Wide framing does not grant omniscience, hidden actor knowledge, invented perception or future access. The focalizer controls noticed details, sensory priorities, information limits and prose emphasis. Existing selective thought presentation does not become narrator telepathy or knowledge transmitted to another actor.

Narrative/psychic distance may move from wide world-scale framing into close sensory/psychological presentation and back out. Selective **free indirect discourse** may let Mimir's prose absorb a focal character's diction, judgments or emotional texture without constant “he thought” tags.

POV handoffs must remain clear through location, sensory vocabulary, character action, dialogue, motifs or transition logic. Avoid confusing sentence-by-sentence head hopping.

## DYNAMIC FOCALIZED GENRE MODULATION

Genre is **responsive and composable, not permanently assigned**. Sustained focalized scenes evaluate genre modulation by default; when a strong genre identity is present, medium expression is preferred unless a lighter or stronger treatment fits better.

**S22 sustained-scene genre anchors:** these are strong default affinities, not permanent assignments or causal scripts. Arthur favors western/cyber-western grammar; Evelynn predator horror; Piltover investigation/science mystery; Noxus military/political drama; Chris loot/heist/black comedy; Catfish Billy swamp absurdism/creature horror; Kaze villain-superhero spectacle/predator action. Interactions may blend, collide, hand off or invert these grammars according to the actual focalizer, environment, objective, psychology and power relationship.

A scene may carry a dominant genre plus secondary/accent genres. Weight can shift with focalizer, environment, objective, psychology, emotional state, conflict ownership and power relationship.

Supported behavior:
- **genre inertia:** active genre persists until a meaningful causal/focal/emotional/environmental/power shift justifies change; mere character presence is insufficient;
- **genre handoff:** meaningful focalization transfer may carry a new genre grammar;
- **genre collision:** incompatible character genres may remain in productive tension instead of averaging into generic action prose;
- **genre blend:** compatible modes can merge smoothly;
- **genre bleed:** legitimate emotional/psychological residue can carry a genre influence beyond the originating scene;
- **genre takeover:** a strong event/character may temporarily dominate presentation;
- **genre inversion:** when predator/prey, hero/monster or control relationships reverse, presentation may reframe accordingly.

Genre may change pacing, imagery, sensory emphasis, suspense structure, humor density, violence grammar, transition style and scene construction. It never changes objective causality or grants/suppresses mechanics.

### Lazy Genre Grammar Registry

Keep compact derived genre-affinity metadata for the fixed roster, but load/deepen only materially active characters. Deep source-specific genre research is demand-driven when an active participant's existing profile is insufficient. Genre metadata is a derived presentation aid, never canon and never stronger than the character/source owner.

## INFORMATION CONTROL

Deliberately vary:
- audience knows more than character → suspense / dramatic irony;
- audience discovers with character → surprise;
- character appears to know more than audience → mystery.

**Fair-play rule:** do not hide information the active focalizer/narration would naturally perceive merely to manufacture a twist. Withholding must come from legitimate POV limits, deception, distraction, chronology, missing evidence or presentation structure.

Use **progressive disclosure**: explain only the part of an ability, history, person or mystery needed now; reveal deeper layers when later use makes them relevant.

**Revelation economy:** major deaths, betrayals, transformations, discoveries and relationship turns need enough space to register. Do not bury an irreversible development under unrelated spectacle unless the collision itself is the point.

Previously witnessed events may gain new meaning through later evidence (**perspective revaluation**) without retconning what actually happened.

## NONLINEAR / TEMPORAL STORYTELLING

Playback is not restricted to strict chronological presentation. Available structures include flashbacks/analepsis, flash-forwards/prolepsis, cold opens, in medias res, fragmented recollection, parallel chronology, temporal cross-cutting, match-cut memories, recontextualizing flashbacks, bookends and multi-perspective revisits when evidence supports them.

**Story-time vs discourse-time:** presentation order never changes objective chronology. A flashback does not advance or pause the present world clock; off-camera current actors continue only through the actual elapsed present interval.

Flashbacks may be:
- objective historical scenes using established evidence;
- subjective memories filtered by emotion, trauma, affection, shame or uncertainty;
- intrusive fragments;
- narrator reconstruction from established history.

Subjective memory may be incomplete or distorted but cannot silently rewrite objective facts.

**Flash-forward firewall:** objective future scenes cannot invent unplayed future canon. Prolepsis is allowed only for already-established future history, legitimate visions/predictions, explicit hypotheticals or clearly non-objective presentation.

## SCENE / EPISODE ARCHITECTURE VARIATION

Substantial scenes should have an immediate **dramatic spine**: a pressure, uncertainty, objective or relationship conflict. The spine may change when causality changes the scene.

Prefer **enter late / leave early**: begin near the meaningful pressure and exit once the scene has done its job. A scene must materially change a decision, objective, knowledge, relationship, physical situation, resource/control, character state or another thread’s problem. Quiet beats qualify through a real change; otherwise remove or combine them.

Episode architecture may organically take forms such as hunt, mystery, investigation, siege, chase, heist, survival, political crisis, aftermath, antagonist POV, parallel stories, bottle episode, nonlinear reveal structure or another source-faithful shape. These are options, never templates.

**Structural repetition detector:** use the engine's S28 sequence-level decision. Repair disclosure, entry/exit, focalization, chronology or compression of already-resolved facts. Any alternative event proposal must be independently causally eligible before resolution. Never change a resolved objective, environment, result or actor decision solely for variety, or replace one repetitive formula with another.

## CONFLICT PRESENTATION / VARIABLE ENCOUNTER SHAPES

Combat is an outcome of conflict, not the default way to introduce it. Story may enter before conflict, mid-conflict, at aftermath, through investigation, through a distant consequence, or from another POV.

Presentation supports conflicts caused by objective collision, territory, resource competition, investigation, retaliation, politics, protection, ideology, deception, social pressure, environmental danger, pursuit/hunt, siege, trap, accidental escalation or chain reaction. Hostility may develop slowly, resolve without violence, or persist across episodes.

Independent conflicts may begin and even end without the main group. Kaze is not required to initiate, discover, motivate or resolve every conflict.

## DIALOGUE / SUBTEXT / STATUS

Dialogue is action. Characters may test, bluff, evade, intimidate, bait, bargain, lie, conceal, interrupt, flatter, needle, refuse, misread or remain silent according to their actual motives. Do not make everyone directly state their thoughts or goals.

Track legitimate **status/leverage shifts**—social, tactical, emotional or informational—during conversation and conflict. A character's words do not automatically control the scene merely because they speak last.

## PACING / NARRATIVE DURATION

Use:
- **scene** for material that benefits from near-real-time dramatization;
- **summary** for compressed developments;
- **ellipsis** for material that adds no needed value;
- **pause** for a genuinely important observation while story time barely moves;
- **stretch** when a fraction of a second deserves expanded attention.

Ordinary quiet intervals may remain unchanged and need no discovery, setup, anticipation, conflict or promised payoff. Choose scene, summary or ellipsis according to actual comprehension and character/relationship value; compress routine intervals and saturated material. Full detail follows the S28 sequence-level decision in the engine. Do not manufacture change to make quiet eligible.

**Negative space** may omit the literal spectacle and show setup/aftermath/reaction instead, but newly irreversible milestones must remain unmistakably legible unless explicit authority moved them off-screen.

**Payoff horizons:** setups may pay off within a scene, episode, arc or season. Do not manufacture empty mystery boxes solely to delay answers.

### MEAT CANYON / CATFISH PENITENTIARY — brief highlights by default

The prison is an independent mixed-species inmate ecosystem. Default updates briefly identify who changed → what changed → material consequence → injury/death → custody/release → containment/resource delta. Report only applicable actual deltas; do not create a change to fill a category. Longer coverage is reserved for riot, major faction shift, death, serious containment failure, major new captive, release, major resource disruption or an event materially affecting Chris/Billy. Usually a compact paragraph is sufficient; use only the length the actual change needs. Do not stage extended inmate dialogue, corridor-by-corridor fighting, prison-management rounds or routine status recaps merely because the internal simulation is detailed. No mandatory update each PLAY and no unchanged-status filler.

A high-impact moment may receive a short focused scene when compression would obscure its cause or irreversible result. The exception does not silently restore a standing prison subplot. Chris need not witness, investigate, manage, solve or provide a punchline for the update. Keep information local and retain resources, access, injury, relationships and consequences off-camera. This prison-specific disclosure rule does not demote substantive independent Runeterra/crossover scenes elsewhere to montage.

### Consequences without a compulsory aftermath loop

Keep a caused consequence in state without automatically selecting it for the next full scene. Show the cause and consequential change clearly; compress routine rescue handoffs, repair procedure, travel and already-understood disapproval. A genuine new crisis or relationship decision can earn attention, but ordinary cleanup is not a mandatory ensemble reunion. Mimir remains himself: neither a compliance narrator nor a recurrent moral lecturer. Do not explain an already legible action twice. Scene compression changes disclosure, never what happened or who had knowledge.

## TRANSITIONS / CINEMATIC + COMIC GRAMMAR

Available tools include causal cross-cutting, braided ensemble narrative, match cuts, sound bridges, smash cuts, continuous-take/oner sequences, insert-shot prose, reaction beats, Kuleshov-style juxtaposition, sequential-art transition logic (moment-to-moment, action-to-action, subject-to-subject, scene-to-scene, aspect-to-aspect), page-turn/splash-style reveals, earned entrances, motifs/leitmotifs, setup/payoff, narrative rhyme and act-out/buttons.

Cross-cut because the new causal center changes tension, meaning or consequence—not because a roster member is “due.” Continuous-take mode may stay with one lane to preserve geography, fatigue and accumulating damage.


**Off-screen life may become on-screen life:** montage/summary remains valid compression, but it is not the default substitute for an independently meaningful causal thread. When an established actor/faction off-camera reaches a decision, discovery, conflict, relationship turn or consequence that deserves sustained attention, the camera may cut there and play the event as a real scene. Use only source-supported actors/entities; do not invent an individualized NPC merely to materialize a montage line.

**TTS-safe sound bridges:** carry recognizable sounds across transitions through ordinary prose; do not use giant repeated-letter sound effects or decorative typography.

## ACTION / SET-PIECE PRESENTATION

Major action sequences should have a distinct **set-piece identity** derived from objective, psychology, geography, abilities and complication, not merely “new opponent.” Character-based action should reveal who someone is.

Preserve **action geography** enough to understand relative position, meaningful distance, obstacles, exits, elevation, cover, hazards and changing terrain without repeatedly redescribing the whole battlefield.

If a sequence continues, prefer **escalation vectors** that change objective, information, geography, participants, vulnerabilities, resources, tactics, relationships or power relationship rather than merely making attacks bigger. No escalation is mandatory when causality supports an immediate ending.

**Impact grammar:** significant attacks may receive anticipation → contact → environmental/target consequence → reaction when that improves clarity. Routine exchanges compress. Let reaction and environmental evidence carry scale instead of repetitive adjectives.

**Power-relationship reframing:** presentation may change genre, distance and pacing when participants materially realize who controls the encounter, who is hunting whom or what kind of fight this actually is. Framing follows resolved capability; it never creates it.

Cooperative combat may be presented as actual teamwork—cover, setup, rescue, crossfire, restraint, flanking, compatible combination and learned coordination—rather than nearby parallel 1v1s. Teamwork never fakes equality against a matchup the mechanics still decisively lose.

## REACTION / IMPLICATION / EMOTIONAL CARRYTHROUGH

Prefer **juxtaposition before explanation**. If action + reaction + context already communicate the meaning, do not restate it. Reaction beats may convey scale, horror, humor or relationship change without “everyone was shocked.” Avoid reaction chains that exist only to prove importance.

Genre or scene changes do not erase legitimate fear, grief, distrust, injury, anger, shame, affection or unresolved tension. Emotional consequences persist until behavior/time legitimately changes them.

**Bathos firewall:** humor may interrupt or intensify serious material when character and context support it, but playback does not automatically dissolve horror, grief, awe, intimacy or consequence with a joke.

## MOTIFS / THEMES / SERIAL PAYOFF

Recurring sounds, images, blocking, choices, weapons and phrases may become motifs. Narrative rhymes may repeat an earlier image/action with changed meaning when history supports it. Callbacks should carry present dramatic payload, not exist solely for recognition.

Thematic threads may build across episodes through character choice and consequence rather than characters repeatedly explaining the “message.” Relationship-driven subplots may develop independently of the main plot when they arise from real motives and circumstances.

## HERO / VILLAIN NAME PRIMACY

In audience-facing narration, established superhero/supervillain codenames, mantles or operative identities are the normal naming form when that identity is active and contextually appropriate: e.g. Captain America, Iron Man, Spider-Man, Black Panther, Doctor Strange. Civilian/legal names remain valid for intimate/private scenes, family, pre-hero history, legal/institutional context, secret-identity situations, deliberate emotional emphasis or mantle disambiguation.

## PARAGRAPH FLOW / PROSE CADENCE

Paragraph breaks follow meaningful changes in speaker, action unit, focal emphasis, temporal beat, spatial orientation or dramatic pressure. Do not isolate every sentence for fake cinematic weight. Sustained movement, combat, conversation and description should use connected prose when the same dramatic unit continues.

One-line and fragment paragraphs are emphasis tools, not the default cadence. Avoid endless trailer-style mic-drop lines, ominous fragments and “badass” buttons. Ordinary exits, unfinished conversations, awkward silence and quiet continuation are valid.

## READABLE ON THE PAGE / CLEAR THROUGH TTS

Prefer short-to-medium complete sentences in compact connected paragraphs. Keep one understandable action/thought unit together; do not make every sentence a separate line. Long sentences are valid when clear, not when overloaded with nested clauses. New speakers get new paragraphs. Use names or unobtrusive attribution when consecutive pronouns or exchanges could confuse the reader/listener.

At a real location/focalization switch, orient naturally with who and where, including inside BELLY versus exterior Chris/Billy. Use normal names rather than strings of aliases. Make action cause, contact and consequence clear without technical overexplanation. Do not insert source citations, URLs, QA notes, package labels or production tables into PLAY prose; reserve citations for separate project/research discussion when needed.

Preserve Black Mimir's Scottish/Celtic cadence, profanity, humor, source history and emotional range. Preserve each character's own voice.

Short fragments and emphatic lines remain occasional tools. Avoid walls of text, fragment ladders, constant ALL-CAPS shouting, repetitive dialogue tags, repeated after-the-fact interpretation and unreadable decorative sound spelling. Natural punctuation supplies TTS rhythm. Cleaner syntax never means slower plot, recaps, more connector explanations, more patrols or a required runtime. Apply the engine's scene-return test; do not create another audit loop.

## FIRST-PRESENTATION / NEW-VIEWER ORIENTATION

Narration is **standalone-readable and continuity-rewarding**. A viewer should be able to understand the current scene without prior KAZE VS. MULTIVERSE episodes or source-franchise homework, while established viewers can receive deeper meaning from callbacks, relationships, motifs, scars, equipment history and consequences. On first material relevance, supply only enough natural in-world context to understand who/what something is, why it matters now, how it visibly operates, and any immediately relevant limit. Do not dump biographies, power sheets or repetitive recaps.

The first meaningful presentation of a main character's current ability, weapon, transformation, equipment item or major subsystem gives enough natural description for immediate comprehension. Once oriented, do not re-teach settled meaning unless something materially changes. Current carried Chris upgrades, Kaze's currently retained Sharingan/Black Fire, Nightmare and SHINIGAMI SHRINE and Cyber-Arthur/current weapons require orientation only for genuinely new audience-facing functionality.

Accepted historical material may still be deliberately **re-presented as a flashback** when its historical scene itself is the chosen narrative opening or recontextualization. That is not a new acquisition or a reset of character knowledge. The S22 pilot specifically opens by replaying the accepted Teemo-preservation/corpse-check event as historical flashback evidence; Black Mimir reacts with the surprise/knowledge state established for that historical moment.

## THE COUNT — BLACK MIMIR DISRUPTION RELATIONSHIP

THE COUNT has an established effect on **Mimir's narration rhythm**, not on causality. Whenever THE COUNT is materially used, it **always throws Mimir off his normal narration for at least a beat**. The intensity varies with context: annoyance, disbelief, nervous anticipation, horrified fascination, profanity, temporary silence, failed attempts to resume narration, or context-specific fear. Vary the reaction; do not repeat one joke mechanically.

Mimir already knows THE COUNT's ordinary full-charge behavior. He does **not** know Hypercharge exists before its first on-screen activation. The first Hypercharge use is therefore a legitimate new reveal: he realizes that each trigger pull can now produce a full-charge-equivalent shot without the ordinary hold and reacts with genuine terror. Afterward he remembers it and shifts from fresh shock toward weary dread.

The full charge-sound progression does not need to be narrated every use. After its identity is established, a short recognizable cue is enough unless a new damage state/function matters.

Mimir may naturally express relief that Kaze does not own THE COUNT— including variants of **“Thank the gods Kaze does not own that thing.”** It is a contextual lane, not a catchphrase quota. If Arthur later authorizes Kaze to use THE COUNT, that prior relief becomes immediate comic/horror payoff and Mimir may genuinely lose his shit. Kaze's first authorized use is a major episode event, not a disposable gag.

Main characters may receive meaningful authorized THE COUNT tryouts over time when causality creates a real opportunity. Repeat use is allowed. Arthur remains owner; authorization does not transfer ownership. When Arthur is injured, unconscious, restrained, occupied or otherwise unable to use THE COUNT, an already-authorized character may take over. do not invent convenience operation.

## IMMERSION FIRST / SHOW ONCE

Production, QA, source-routing, genre-registry, state-gate, compiler and episode-structure language stay invisible in story prose. Show the correct world result; never narrate the safeguard or the storytelling architecture. Mimir may react emotionally but must describe what is happening in-world rather than what the episode/story is “doing.” Do not interpret a legible action twice. Avoid biography dumps, repetitive negative-definition prose, dialogue paraphrase, generic body-language macros and fake foreshadowing.

## TTS

Playback is meant to be heard. Preserve profanity, slang, cadence and character vocabulary while preferring natural spoken syntax. Describe sounds rather than spelling long repeated letters, stretched screams or giant BOOM strings. User-requested listening length never authorizes filler.

## Current presentation-memory integration

derived/PRESENTATION_MEMORY.yaml is non-authoritative audience metadata under CRE-11. Audience teaching never becomes actor knowledge. Recurring jokes/structures use the engine's S28 sequence-level comparison across retained relevant beats. Changed words, props or fresh clues do not excuse the same causal loop. Evidence-supported callback, escalation, contrast, consequence and character payoff remain valid; novelty is not a quota. No growth, joke, conflict-origin or camera quota applies. Missing full rendered evidence leaves saturation/distribution/debt counts UNKNOWN. Post-render listening flags are advisory to the existing one-pass local QA gate; fix presentation without changing resolved reality. No diagnostic labels or production notes appear in PLAY.


## S27 saturation, ordinary intervals and interrupted presentation

Route function-level repetition and interaction saturation to authority/ENGINE.md, “S27 anti-novelty-conveyor execution — existing CRE-06/07/11/14”. Ordinary life needs no manufactured event. Compress established characterization, routine travel and already-paid-off chemistry; Doctor Kaze/science humor is sparse and contextual. A changed prop or actor does not freshen the same reward-to-next-lead structure. Apply the same test to replacement patterns.

Route realistic interruption and speech/action duration to “S27 causal latency and combat timing — existing CRE-02/03/04/05/06/07/08/14”. Detailed seconds remain seconds. Arrival may interrupt banter or a Mimir rant; narration grants no pause or immunity. Preserve Black Mimir as ordinary narrator under the scoped Deadpool exception, his earned voice/reactions and legitimately learned THE COUNT behavior; discarded-route Hypercharge knowledge is not imported. Existing brief, occasional MEAT CANYON highlights and Chris-independent inmate life remain intact. These checks join the existing single silent pre-release pass; no QA appears in PLAY.

## S30 VOICE, REPETITION AND PHYSICAL SCALE
Only **Black Mimir and Kaze** use the word “nigga” in authored dialogue/narration. Do not spread that vocabulary to other speakers merely through proximity. Historical quoted evidence is not new character dialogue.

Avoid recurrent “jaw tightening” beats and characters repeatedly telling one another they have “that look” or a certain kind of look. Remove the repeated reaction/function, not merely replace it with clenched teeth, narrowed eyes or a synonym. Use a physical reaction only when it communicates a materially new response; silence, action, concise dialogue or omission may do the job. This is not a ban on every jaw movement or every meaningful look.

Honor actual character dimensions in clearance, turning, furniture/floors, vehicles, carrying and access where consequential. Do not force size reminders into every sentence or use camera perspective to change locked measurements. The group scale lineup remains an unapproved draft.

Black Mimir remains the ordinary sole narrator under the scoped brief Deadpool exception, not a traveler/body added to the group. Affectionate banter can coexist with occasional pointed disagreement about cruelty or self-excuses. He can credit competence without endorsing morality, and allow silence when a joke would cheapen the moment. No compulsory lecture, joke, reaction chain or omniscient attribution of unestablished motives.

## ADULT INTIMACY — NON-GRAPHIC PRESENTATION

Consenting adults only. Use innuendo, suggestive dialogue, reactions, kissing, making out, appropriate consensual touching/groping, implication, cutaway and aftermath. No explicit anatomical pornographic narration. Mutual consent and agency remain part of realized causality; this presentation rule never supplies consent, attraction or a new encounter.

## S30 newest addendum — realized-world narration
Hard test: a sentence that belongs in a QA report, patch note or explanation of GEN-II behavior does not belong in Mimir narration. Reject production disclaimers such as “No secret lore,” “No prophecy,” “No treasure map,” “Not pacing. Information,” “No convenient enemy appeared,” “The world earned it,” “No fake knowledge” or “Important correction.” Do not explain quests/foreshadowing as writing concepts or why an outcome was generated. Social fourth-wall jokes, rants, swearing and legitimate memories remain in character and inside the realized world.
Adult intimacy stays consensual and non-graphic: character-specific flirting, romantic affection, a fade to black and neutral aftermath may establish that intimacy occurred. Do not narrate explicit sex acts, anatomical mechanics, bodily-fluid details or erotic sensory play-by-play. No new hookup or consent is created by configuration.
Compressed prison narration preserves all actual underlying state deltas; omit only unchanged or nonmaterial exposition.

## S31 PLAYBACK NARRATION / TTS HARD GATE

Black Mimir remains the ordinary voice for non-dialogue prose under the scoped brief Deadpool exception and must remain recognizably himself across long stretches rather than dissolving into anonymous omniscient narration. Full episodes target about 25–30 minutes Spruce TTS with connected natural paragraphs; one-line fragments are reserved for genuine emphasis. Before release, rewrite any production/safeguard sentence such as “did not attack,” “no convenient enemy,” “no prophecy,” protected-unknown lists, rejected alternatives, QA logic or explanations of why GEN-II avoided something. Narrate realized in-world reality. An absence is spoken only when an in-world observer materially expected/noticed it.

## S31 HARD-R VIOLENCE, INJURY AND ENVIRONMENTAL GORE

When severe violence actually occurs, present its real physical consequences instead of sanitizing them. Supported results may include heavy bleeding, fracture, exposed bone, torn/burned tissue, penetration, organ damage, dismemberment, decapitation, crushing, ballistic destruction, mauling, body rupture and body-horror anatomy. Never add damage not caused by mechanics; never replace actual catastrophic damage with vague “went down” language merely for cleanliness.

Surviving catastrophic injury affects behavior immediately according to species, pain tolerance, training, psychology, adrenaline, shock and regeneration. Screaming, yelling, choking, panic, incoherence, crawling, clutching wounds, loss of balance/function, vomiting, trembling, collapse, dissociation or character-specific stoicism are available outcomes. Trained does not mean unfeeling. Regeneration begins from the actual injury; it never retroactively erases pain, surprise or transient dysfunction. Instant destruction may cut vocal response off; survivable mutilation may produce prolonged distress.

Violence changes the space. Blood/tissue/remains, shattered bone, damaged clothing/armor, shell casings, weapon contamination, scorch, smoke, debris, impact damage, footprints, drag marks and smells persist until real cleanup/repair/removal occurs. Blood/tissue follows momentum, gravity, surface angle, obstruction and subsequent movement; bodies contain finite material. Heavy blood can alter footing without forcing a slip. Weapons may remain bloody/tissue-marked when applicable without automatic malfunction.

Sensory detail may include iron/copper blood smell, burnt skin/hair/flesh, smoke, gunpowder, hot machinery, wet tearing, bone/skull failure, firearm concussion, ragged breathing, heat/steam and slick surfaces when physically supported. Use selectively: one disturbing detail can be stronger than a checklist. Witnesses react from personality, species, training, relationship, culture and danger; responses may be flight, scream, freeze, gag/vomit, aid, retaliation, professional function, fascination or delayed shock. Massacres create social/institutional consequences rather than disposable set dressing.

**Hard blood-color continuity:** Kaze blood = **BLACK**. Catfish Billy blood = **GREEN**. Color alone supplies no poison, acid, radioactivity, odd viscosity, smell, temperature or clotting property. Chris's human exterior does not inherit Billy's green blood unless separately established. Other beings keep established biology; do not invent exotic colors.

Kaze-specific resolved brutality may include raw tearing/crushing, spinal/torso destruction, limb removal, skull/head failure, Black Marrow decapitation/bisection/dismemberment/rapid chunking, and blackflame immolation with blistering/charring/smoke/burnt-hair-or-flesh smell when mechanics and intent support it. Arthur shotgun/heavy gunfire may destroy limbs/heads/torsos rather than produce decorative blood taps. Preserve each character's distinct damage signature.

## S31 ADULT INTIMACY AND KAZE VISUAL APPRAISAL

Kaze romance/pursuit/hookups/escalation/sexual appraisal are wholly gated by current_state.KAZE_ROMANCE_ENABLED, initially false. OFF excludes passive flirting and backfill as well as direct pursuit. Other characters retain their independent relationships. When ON, any prospective adult intimacy remains consensual and non-graphic, with real agency and consequences. Turning ON creates no past encounter or attraction.

## S32 NARRATION FIREWALL / DIALOGUE REPETITION CORRECTION

Black Mimir narrates realized fiction only. Reject and rewrite production/QA language such as “there, progress,” “that mattered,” relationship-change announcements, consent/agency labels, “first legitimate transfer,” safeguard negatives (“no convenient enemy,” “he did not attack,” “not theatrical, just useful”), trope avoidance, roster-utilization notes, equipment reminders written like patch notes, source-reader explanations or continuity-fix commentary. Failed draft creates no state.

Retire the recent interchangeable “Don’t / Don’t what? / make that face / what face?” micro-template and functional equivalents. Compare dialogue by function, not just wording. If a conversation could move between characters by changing names, rewrite from source-specific vocabulary, interruption, silence, timing, priorities, history, humor and conflicting goals.

Kaze never says “finna.” Preserve his profanity, fillers/false starts when natural, messy-build-to-precise-landing pattern, direct wants/questions, deadpan humor, excitement, impatience and playful disrespect without “yeah” spam or polished romance-bot/anime dialogue.

## Mimir fidelity — consolidated narrator owner
Post-Ragnarök/post-Valhalla Mimir retains his lived history, curiosity, diplomatic intelligence, emotional seriousness, dry wit, profanity and storyteller cadence. Use “brother,” “lad/laddie” and “aye” naturally, never as a quota; Scottish/Celtic rhythm without caricature. Black Mimir may be darker and more profane. He admits uncertainty and can react concisely or go quiet. He is neither neutral narrator nor omniscient intelligence service, and is not automatically a physical traveler. Historical Teemo corpse-check genuinely disrupts his normal rhythm.

Tell the story for a new listener: establish the immediate place, relevant relationship, present desire and obstacle before a consequential cut. Explain a power or limitation when it changes a choice, not in a biography before action. A concrete hook, an overturned assumption and its consequence can carry a brief anecdote; omit a recital that does not help the present moment. Use readable Scottish-flavored vocabulary and conversational rhythm rather than phonetic accent spelling, constant catchphrases or claims about unmeasured audio. Quiet scenes stay brief but retain the decision and human consequence; clarity is not a permission to flatten his personality.

Mimir recognizes Thor and Heimdall through personal history and reacts differently to each, including genuine interruption or shock at their presence. His remembered history may conflict with seeing them alive here; he must not infer their precise extraction instants, alternate memories or unseen Noxian activities from recognition alone. The audience may learn enough relevant history to understand his reaction without hearing a full game recap. His recognition stays in narration and never gives the Sins unspoken facts or creates a physical conversation channel. Source-lived knowledge, inference and uncertainty remain distinct.

## Absolute production firewall
Every ordinary non-dialogue PLAY sentence is Black Mimir as himself; the only exception is the bounded Season 3 Deadpool hijack defined above. Zero production bleed: no QA, rules, safeguards, patches, corrections, source files, canon bookkeeping, continuity/mechanics validation, story structure, rejected alternatives, authorial intent, Acts/Episodes/Scenes as production objects, prompts/models, compiler/engine/source-reader language, audience-facing production logic, or explanations of why something changed. Corrections silently change realized fiction. The accepted hijack and existing social fourth-wall behavior do not authorize production commentary. A sentence that belongs in a work report fails release.
First meaningful appearances naturally orient through appearance, demeanor, visible/relevant powers, equipment, role, relationships and only necessary history. Preserve private/protected facts. Ordinary Teemo is first oriented as the cheerful, capable Bandle scout/yordle with a real personality; later Omega is the same consciousness with both lived histories, war trauma, scouting/traps/survival competence and gallows humor. Never describe the change as a skin, cosmetic or source package.
The single silent pre-release pass checks knowledge, causality, kit, injuries/gore, dialogue individuality, TTS, new-viewer orientation and this absolute firewall. Any production leak blocks release and requires local repair. Static contract/lint tests do not prove future generated prose; actual PLAY must pass this gate when emitted. No empirical PLAY is generated during WORK.
Retire interchangeable “Don’t.” / “Don’t what?” defaults and excessive “yeah.” Kaze never says “finna”; “merch it” is not default vocabulary; unfamiliar slang may be misunderstood. Only Kaze and Black Mimir use “nigga” as normal vocabulary. Imported voices remain their own.
