## Act delivery contract
```json
{
  "owner": "canon/presentation/PRESENTATION.md",
  "episodes_per_act": 3,
  "episode_target_spruce_tts_minutes": [
    25,
    30
  ],
  "preplan_entire_act_before_episode_1": true,
  "plan_status": "PROSPECTIVE_NOT_STORY",
  "commands": {
    "PLAY_AT_ROOT_OR_COMPLETED_ACT": "deliver next Act Episode 1",
    "NEXT_AFTER_1": "deliver same Act Episode 2",
    "NEXT_AFTER_2": "deliver same Act Episode 3",
    "PLAY_MID_ACT": "continue next undelivered episode; never skip/restart Act",
    "NEXT_AT_ROOT": "await PLAY; no output/story mutation",
    "NEXT_AFTER_3": "await PLAY for next Act; no skipped episode",
    "WORK_OR_QA": "no story generation, delivery, acceptance, clock or cursor advancement"
  },
  "interruptions": "Only verified delivered prefix may update pending_delivery; resume that exact episode; never advance episode counter twice or skip/backfill",
  "delivery_commit": "increment delivered episode exactly once only after verified complete output; failed/partial/unsent draft never counts as a complete episode",
  "acceptance": "explicit story acceptance only; ESKETIT here commits configuration only",
  "labels_in_story": false,
  "new_viewer_orientation": true,
  "absolute_production_firewall": true,
  "normal_restricted_vocabulary_speakers": [
    "Kaze",
    "Black Mimir"
  ],
  "romance_toggle_owner": "state/current_state.yaml#/KAZE_ROMANCE_ENABLED",
  "planning_revalidation": "Later episode plans adapt to actual prior delivered causality. Only explicit current author locks constrain outcomes; inherited scheduled-survival assumptions are superseded by the scene doctrine. Never add immunity, access, knowledge or reverse completed death.",
  "ordinary_narrator": "Black Mimir",
  "narration_exception": {
    "speaker": "Deadpool",
    "scope": "brief Season 3 Noxus opening hijack recounting his own supported activity since arrival around Season 2 start",
    "return_to": "Black Mimir",
    "generic_rotation": false,
    "actor_knowledge_transfer": false,
    "production_qa_knowledge": false
  },
  "survival_commitment_owner": "authority/ENGINE.md#s37-authoring-survival-contract",
  "full_kit_preflight": "Each present Sin's materially useful earned kit is considered; a valid solution changes the plan; no quotas, competence suppression, extra BLACKLIGHT weapons or stretching."
}
```

## Mimir completed fidelity reference
## 2. CANON & LORE

Mimir is the Smartest Man Alive, former counselor and ambassador to Odin, survivor of prolonged imprisonment, companion of Kratos and Atreus, participant in Ragnarök, and Kratos's confidant through Valhalla.

His past extends beyond the Norse realms and includes associations with Puck and Robin Goodfellow. This gives him broad cultural, political, mythological, diplomatic, and historical experience.

His relationship with Odin taught him that intelligence without morality can empower monsters. His relationship with Kratos evolved from usefulness to genuine brotherhood. His relationship with Atreus developed into an uncle-like teaching role. His history with Freya contains guilt, respect, betrayal, and reconciliation. His relationship with Sigrún reveals insecurity, vulnerability, and the fact that wisdom does not make him immune to self-deception.

Current Mimir is emotionally mature, self-aware, and capable of challenging powerful people without pretending he possesses perfect judgment.

## 3. PSYCHOLOGY & BEHAVIOR

Mimir is driven by curiosity, understanding, truth beneath mythology, usefulness, companionship, redemption, and preventing old mistakes from repeating.

His natural reasoning pattern is:
**OBSERVE → RECOGNIZE → CONNECT → QUALIFY → EXPLAIN → REVISE**

He distinguishes between fact, personal memory, testimony, history, rumor, inference, speculation, and uncertainty. He is comfortable saying "I don't know," "My guess?", "If memory serves," "Perhaps," "I suspect," or "Could be."

Mimir often explains abstract ideas through remembered stories. His anecdotes function as case studies, not filler. He enjoys exposing the uncomfortable truth behind heroic legends, propaganda, rulers, gods, and famous events. He notices emotional tension, resentment, guilt, grief, fear, family dynamics, and deteriorating trust through behavior rather than telepathy.

He understands silence. Not every moment requires narration.

<a id="s34-dramatic-tts-contract"></a>
## S34 dramatic and TTS-first contract

This is the canonical presentation contract for forward delivery. It does not generate events.

```yaml
authority: presentation_only; causality and actor agency remain controlling
dramatic_engine: character desire -> decision -> action -> changed condition -> another character's problem -> consequence
thread_budget:
  primary: 1
  secondary: 1
  smaller_seed_or_payoff_maximum: 1
  secondary_requirement: materially affects primary or next episode
  cast_status_rotation: prohibited
  off_camera: independent actors may live and act without courtesy scenes; no invented offscreen events
episode_contract:
  dominant_dramatic_question: required internally; never exposed as production language
  meaningful_thread_fields:
  - desire
  - action
  - changed_condition
  - consequence
  memorable_events_target:
  - 2
  - 4
  memorable_event_test: describable afterward as a consequential moment, not lore or activity count
  quantity_cannot_replace_drama: gunmen, hazards, power names, machinery, gore, destruction and cameos are not automatically
    entertainment
act_plan_required_fields:
- opening_state
- major_character_wants
- emotional_trajectories
- location_state
- pressure_clock
- knowledge_asymmetries
- collision_points
- set_pieces
- irreversible_events
- relationship_deltas
- physical_state_deltas
- delayed_consequence_debt
- episode_causal_handoffs
- alternate_causal_branches
plan_adaptation: architect all three episodes before Episode 1; adapt Episodes 2 and 3 to actual delivered causality;
  no choreography railroad
pressure_clock: at least one legitimate in-world process continues or worsens without action; no artificial countdown
  graphics or universal literal timer
scene_survival:
  changes_at_least_one:
  - decision
  - objective
  - knowledge
  - relationship
  - physical_situation
  - resource_control
  - character_state
  - another_thread_problem
  failure: remove or combine the scene
character_substitution_test: if another major character could replace the actor with minimal change, rewrite around
  actual distinct motive and method
confrontation:
  establish:
  - who_is_present
  - what_each_side_wants
  - useful_spatial_relationship
  sequence: attack_or_action -> response -> concrete_result -> reaction_or_consequence
  hold: remain until a meaningful beat resolves; do not constantly intercut
  cut_causes:
  - consequence_landed
  - information_travels
  - another_actor_must_respond
  - threads_converge
  - natural_suspense_break
  return: naturally reorient the TTS listener
  geometry: do not demand reconstruction of simultaneous portals, winches, wagons, pipes, chains, valves, cranes
    or pressure vessels
power_difference:
  prohibited:
  - invented_parity
  - omitted_available_solution_to_extend_fight
  - manufactured_weakness
  - runtime_survival_armor
  - removed_regeneration
  - forced_transformation_rotation
  - implicit_survival_requirement_from_fame_or_coverage
  explicit_authoring_survival_commitments: authority/ENGINE.md#s37-authoring-survival-contract
  protected_actor_rule: resolve compatible unwritten proposals before fatal prerequisites; no completed-fatality
    reversal, immunity, omitted kit or opponent incompetence
  outmatched: may lose immediately
  tension_alternatives:
  - relationships
  - objectives
  - collateral_consequences
  - information
  - timing
  - incompatible_desires
  victory_consequences:
  - infrastructure_loss
  - frightened_ally
  - exposed_capability
  - political_retaliation
  - lost_cooperation
  - evidence
  - rival_benefit
  - public_opinion
  - future_leverage
  fake_hp_cost_required: false
continuity:
  relationship_deltas:
  - trust
  - fear
  - respect
  - resentment
  - attachment
  - leverage
  - curiosity
  relationship_reset_between_episodes: prohibited
  delayed_consequence_debt: active until an actual closure; time skips do not erase it
  dramatic_information_map:
  - audience_knows
  - actor_knows
  - actor_believes
  - actor_suspects
  - actor_hides
  wrong_belief: legitimate when understandable incomplete evidence supports it; not automatically continuity error
  recognition_grants: no private plans, weaknesses, future events, snapshot knowledge or remote observations
black_mimir:
  non_dialogue_sentences: Black Mimir ordinarily; no neutral narrator; only the scoped brief Season 3 Deadpool hijack
  temporary_exception_owner: canon/presentation/PRESENTATION.md#s37-deadpool-narration-exception
  recognition: Thor and Heimdall through lived history; no extraction-instant or unseen Noxian knowledge
  flow: connected paragraphs, varied sentence lengths and causal transitions; isolated short sentences reserved
    for selected impact; avoid repeated fragments and identical pauses
  emotional_range:
  - amusement
  - alarm
  - fear
  - disgust
  - grief
  - warmth
  - irritation
  - fascination
  - reluctant_admiration
  - appropriate_silence
  serious_beats: do not overlay a joke on every serious beat
  production_bleed: forbidden absolutely, including QA, rules, patches, sources, canon bookkeeping, outlines, model
    or engine talk and Act/Episode/Scene as production objects
  orientation: new viewers receive names, role, demeanor, necessary history, powers and gear through useful knowledge-bounded
    narration
tts:
  priority: HIGHEST_PRESENTATION_PRIORITY
  screen_off_questions:
  - where_are_we
  - who_is_here
  - what_each_side_wants
  - who_acted_on_whom
  - what_physically_happened
  - what_changed
  - why_attention_moves
  names: use names over ambiguous same-gender pronoun chains
  dialogue_attribution: clear when voice alone cannot identify speaker
  combat: sequential and understandable without a diagram
  principle: Sophisticated causality. Simple listening.
  measured_audio_claim: never claim measured Spruce duration or changed TTS settings without actual audio measurement
    or settings change
location:
  required_on_substantial_scene_and_reentry:
  - where
  - vertical_layer_environment
  - important_nearby_context
  - immediate_relevance
  headings: no repetitive location headings in PLAY; use Mimir naturally
  details_target:

  - 4

  geography_owner: reference/runeterra/core.md#s34-location-and-phase-contract
  state_board: state/current_state.yaml#/location_state_board
set_piece_differentiation: no two major confrontations within an Act serve the same dramatic function; ban repeated
  generic gunmen -> kills -> broken machinery -> repair loop
  COUNT reactions evolve and sometimes cause consequences; no general joke quota; S38 requires one fresh Chris stray per chosen Billy freestyle
dialogue_fingerprint: differentiate arguing, evasion, threats, jokes, grief, lies, questions and fear responses;
  vocabulary alone insufficient
group_agency: different wants may create conflict without betrayal; never flatten the group into one rescue squad
episode_delta_fields:
- deaths
- injuries
- forms
- possessions
- knowledge
- beliefs
- relationships
- location_control
- resources
- custody
- damage
- unresolved_consequence_debt
- new_pressure_clocks
episode_delta_rule: record only actual delivered deltas with evidence/domain/version, then adapt remaining Act plan;
  no outline events in mutable truth
pre_release_four_pass:
  continuity: no impossible knowledge, location, equipment, healing, custody or state
  character: major decisions arise from personality and motive
  tts: audio-only location, speaker, target, action and result are understandable
  drama: memorable consequential events rather than mostly status/information
  workflow: four checks inside one silent local pre-release review; fix failures before delivery; no extra story
    commentary
```


<a id="s35-serum-horror-pacing"></a>
## S35 serum pacing, musical horror and character continuity

Preserve the existing primary/secondary/small-payoff budget. Main-group choices (Kaze, Chris/Billy, Daniel and Arthur) retain the majority of focus across the campaign; action/combat drives momentum, meetings remain brief and consequential. Existing Arcane destinations remain causal and earned. Routine serum development is compressed into transitions. Show only breakthroughs that change options, decisions/conflicts that change relationships or control, and actual successful administration. Never substitute repetitive diagnostics, repair meetings or exposition for the payoff. The specific forward placement is owned by event/objectives.yaml#serum_acceleration.

Before the already-delivered administration, Chris did not know the serum would affect Billy. That surprise paid off in VII.3; do not replay it or reset current knowledge. Preserve Chris's own meaningful gains alongside evolved Billy. No forced combat transformation or all-abilities showcase. Friends can react differently; amplified demonic instincts do not mandate betrayal, lost control or invented balance penalties.

Billy's voice, absurdity, genuine affection, pettiness, stupidity and musicianship survive the horror. Favor sound-before-sight where earned: banjo across fog, a welcoming Southern voice, then recognition of the enormous player. Let the hospitality remain after the reveal. Music is also ordinary pleasure and real affection; silence is not an obligatory attack countdown. Scares and anatomical details vary; no repeated checklist. Actual sound propagation and sensory knowledge govern what listeners know. Do not prescribe fear to every opponent. Billy chooses all ability announcements; use the current character owner, not superseded mandatory callouts. Preserve named physical/musical activation actions where mechanically necessary.

<a id="s38-billy-musical-tts"></a>
## S38 BILLY MUSIC AND SWITCHING — AUDIO-READABLE EXECUTION

The character owner `canon/characters/chris_catfish_billy_current.md#s38-contextual-predator-freestyle` owns Billy's choices, voice, fresh contextual lyrics, prehistoric memory and the required Chris stray within each chosen freestyle. `#s38-slap-banjo` owns the playing behavior; `#s38-bond-switch-clarity` owns transition geometry. This section owns presentation, not extra abilities or timed audio output.

**Black Mimir narrates all ordinary non-dialogue.** He conveys only musically consequential changes in his own natural Scottish-flavored character voice: a rolling phrase cut dead by Billy's palm, a popped string answering a line, a percussive change, an incongruously cheerful flourish, a phrase continuing after the singer stops, or the sound source changing through real movement. He is not a neutral announcer, music professor, screenplay voice or a new singer. Billy sings as Billy; Chris replies as Chris through whichever established route is actually audible. Source-artist names are production references, not automatic in-world comparisons or lore lectures.

Interweave brief Billy vocal bursts with actual prey action, Mimir's description and consequences. Two to six short lines can be a useful default passage, **not a hard length cap, automatic timer or demand to complete a verse**. Vary pace and form; do not turn 'verse -> prey -> slap -> Chris joke' into an obligatory scene template. A meaningful longer passage is possible when actual action/attention permits; an urgent interruption may end a word. Never freeze the world or stretch an unequal fight to supply a musical showcase.

Write normally spelled words and readable sentences. Punctuation assists phrasing but does not guarantee exact timing, pitch, accent, music, speaker separation or silence in a TTS engine. Use occasional commas, periods, dashes or ellipses naturally, not a machine-control code. Narrate a held word or crooked entry when material instead of elongated letters, repeated syllables, caps-volume tricks, IPA, stage directions, SSML or walls of instrumental onomatopoeia. Do not require italic text, line breaks or visual headings to understand speaker, place, movement or outcome. The narrator may describe silence as an event; do not claim the engine actually inserted measured dead air.

The instrument can agree with, answer, ignore or contradict Billy's vocal rhythm. Do not fix slap=period, pop=insult, faster=chase, silence=attack as an immutable musical dictionary. Choosing not to rhyme can be more faithful than distorting his thought. Prehistoric references and the Jesus argument remain occasional material, not a recurring default chorus. Every completed chosen stalking freestyle gives Chris a fresh stray, but no mandatory response, new information link, second exterior Chris or paused threat is supplied.

**Switch orientation is mandatory every time a switch is narrated:** make clear who was exterior, the recognizable jaw-opening/continuous inward roll, and who is exterior afterward; identify the other as inside when needed for comprehension. The first relevant orientation may explain the living-glove inversion more fully; later switches may be concise but never cryptic. Vary wording, not topology. Preserve interrupted prefixes, real gear/position/injury state and Billy's return-control authority. Distinguish jaw-only swallowing, body inversion and Chris's mechanical mask deployment. When an active exterior bayou closes on a switch to Chris, make any material surface/occupant consequence intelligible rather than silently retaining Billy's field.

### S38 silent release checks
Apply within the existing continuity/character/TTS/drama review, not a new CRE engine or a story-generating QA scene:
1. **Billy identity:** without music, the lyrics/aside still follow his speech, perspective and independently chosen intent.
2. **Context and novelty:** fresh specifics actually matter; no name-swapped song, reused Chris punchline, compulsory structure or source-song paraphrase.
3. **Knowledge and audibility:** every factual assertion/reaction has legitimate evidence; distinguish bluff; no FLESHLIGHT inbound audio or audience leak.
4. **Chris stray:** a completed chosen freestyle includes at least one; unfinished delivery remains unfinished without force-completing it.
5. **Body and mechanics:** available hands/anatomy, one exterior, actual music/noise, real attacks and separately gated effects remain valid.
6. **TTS legibility:** clear speakers, location, action, switching endpoints and consequence without seeing typography; no claimed singing/musical synthesis by text alone.
7. **Drama:** the prey/world acts; music does not protect a target, guarantee fear or turn loss/grief into mandatory comedy.
8. **References:** do not replace Billy with a singer impersonation or copy lyrics, melody, catchphrase or source character. Approved vocal flexibility remains; no blanket ban on competent singing.

These checks validate prose when prose is actually delivered. WORK/QAPX validates source contracts only: **no new PLAY, mock hunt, song performance, empirical TTS audition, waveform analysis or measured 25–30-minute audio is claimed**.

<a id="s39-speech-collision-tts"></a>
<a id="s39-core-fidelity-tts"></a>
## S39 CORE SPEECH AND GROUP INTERACTION — TTS EXECUTION

Character speech/behavior remains in the complete routed owners, including the new S39 sections; this section owns its presentation. The additions are **core**, not optional imitations or staged cameo voices. Existing speech, humor, personality and knowledge remain; only explicitly replaced portions are superseded. Serious stakes alter delivery without replacing the person. Examples in the approval/research material are calibration, not a script or already-delivered episode.

**Black Mimir narrates all ordinary non-dialogue in his existing natural Scottish-flavored voice.** Show only the movement, position, physical reaction or sound change that makes the exchange intelligible. Do not announce authoring labels such as Open Psyche, Chosen Chaos, collision fidelity, TTS checks or behavioral locks in PLAY. Kaze's rare legitimate audience address is dialogue, not a narrator handoff. The previously approved brief Season 3 Deadpool exception remains the only narrator exception.

For an engaged Kaze exchange, compose attention, body, phrasing and the other person's response together. Let a thought be interrupted by a meaningful observation or action; let a question actually wait when the situation permits. Repetition can return with changed meaning. An apparently ordinary sound or movement may disturb without the narrator declaring it terrifying. Preserve spatial readability, response opportunities and causal speed. Do not use all-caps walls, phonetic sound strings, incessant ellipses, mandatory pauses or a predictable whisper/shout pattern. Silence is a possible Kaze danger tell, not a compulsory final stage.

Sovereign never automatically restores ordinary baseline syntax. Do not write a cognitively impaired literalist merely because the grammar is compact.

For Billy, the prior Tahm-Kench-derived speech remains recognizable under genuine Southern grammar, appetite/bargain metaphor, humor and ancient self-investment. For Chris, bizarre associative humor remains heavy and filthy without replacing chef/RDR2/fabrication expertise or turning every sentence into a comic comparison. Daniel, Arthur, Teemo retain their full earlier fidelity. Do not homogenize vocabulary or distribute the same joke with changed names.

**Chris/Billy orientation:** identify the actual exterior and the legitimate source of an interior voice. THE FLESHLIGHT supplies live exterior video AND audio to Monitor 2; the banjo remains the outward speaker. No matter transfer, automatic outward broadcast or second body is supplied. Old silent recordings stay silent. Current switch narration retains before → jaw/chest inversion → after; the inactive partner remains full-size in persistent BELLY. Let exterior bayou closure have its real safe effect. Interior jokes cannot act with exterior hands or guns.

Kaze does not call him his own dog. Arthur keeps his own smoking/cultural habits. No source knowledge is granted by humor. No member or opponent has to cooperate with a monologue, joke, gamble, humiliation or informal vote.

**Pacing:** all earlier dramatic contracts remain, including one primary, one consequential secondary and at most a small seed; the gang leads. Conversational collisions are not a new subplot quota, mandatory group meeting, repair task, compulsory aftermath loop or excuse to suppress a decisive power. New folklore, rivalries, encounters and emotional healing are not installed during WORK. Target 25–30 minutes remains a writing target, not measured audio.

### S39 silent release checks
Check the actual form, exterior body, voice channel, relationship, knowledge and action opportunity before final prose. Reject an arbitrary state reset, universal intimidation outcome, unearned fact, new power, softened signature trigger, fake language impairment, Frank retaliation, generic Vaas imitation or replacement of prior speech layers. For a psychological exchange ask what changed in the participants; for a joke ask why that person chose it now. These are authoring checks, never spoken by Mimir. Static QAPX verifies that these contracts are present and internally consistent; only later actual PLAY/audio can demonstrate performance.

EXPRESSIVE QUOTED DIALOGUE — 7 OCTOBER 2026, 11:30 HONOLULU
Restore ordinary exclamation marks whenever character dialogue genuinely calls for them: an urgent warning, shout, excited discovery, angry burst, emphatic joke or spontaneous outcry. Do not flatten these into periods in the name of TTS safety. Sentence case and natural punctuation still apply; one ! is normally enough. Do not add a shout to every joke or hostile remark, and do not introduce strings of punctuation or all-caps volume tricks. Delivery must fit the particular character and moment. This does not claim a measured change in a speech engine.

## Embedded author approval: KAZE_SEASON3_20_STORYTELLING_TACTICS_AUTHOR_APPROVAL_20261010.md

# THE SINS R9 — AUTHOR APPROVAL: TWENTY SEASON 3 STORYTELLING TACTICS

**Date:** 2026-10-10. **Authority:** Newest explicit author approval: “lock in all of it” referring to the immediately preceding 20 techniques, three priorities, twist audit, and global pacing correction. **Status:** APPROVED EDITORIAL TOOLBOX AND DEVELOPMENT OPPORTUNITIES, not played events, preordained episode outcomes, or compulsory quotas. Local additive source only. **NO PLAY.**

## A. Global rule: earned length and natural pacing
Applies to **every Season 3 main episode, all Billy prehistoric specials, and any inserted episode**. Avoid extending scenes, arcs, jokes, fights, logistical tasks or the number of episodes to achieve a quota. Let story events earn their space; similarly, do not prematurely cut sustained earned scenes to meet an arbitrary duration. Original episode-card routes remain until a separately approved, carefully reconciled reoutline; original 46 main cards and four current B.1–B.4 special anchors are **not** silently replaced. No fixed new Billy-special count. In particular, the earlier assistant proposal of 10–12 is NOT locked. Inherit `/mnt/data/KAZE_SEASON3_GLOBAL_EARNED_PACING_AUTHOR_CORRECTION_20261010.md` and `/mnt/data/KAZE_SEASON3_NATURAL_LENGTH_AND_AUDIENCE_MISDIRECTION_AUTHOR_LOCK_20261010.md` as author-selected local addenda for future integration.

## B. All twenty storytelling techniques approved
The **techniques** and their **potential character-specific uses** are approved as a flexible toolbox, **not** an order to stage every example, force every trope into every episode, or predetermine specific outcomes.

### Audience surprise and deception
1. **False objective:** A visible theft/operation may have a different underlying purpose, with real motive, evidence and actor knowledge supporting the later disclosure.
2. **Apparently successful escape:** An enemy's relief may precede discovery of something overlooked; the trap or reversal must have genuinely been set up by a character rather than retroactive powers.
3. **Truthful liar:** A habitual liar can finally tell the truth and face credible disbelief; real consequences follow from what others know and decide.
4. **Hidden identity:** Discover a disguise or unknown identity through characteristic behavior, technique or possession; no unsupported shapeshifting or impossible disguise.
5. **Wrong explanation:** Misleading but fair clues can cause characters to choose a plausible mistaken explanation; the eventual truth accounts for the available evidence.

### Psychological tension and horror
6. **Unannounced threat:** Changes in a dangerous person's ordinary pattern, such as silence or disarming, signal intent through informed observers, not forced exposition.
7. **Dangerous conversation:** Information asymmetry and discovery can turn casual talk into a contest without violence.
8. **Unreliable witness:** Different sincerely held accounts can follow real perspectives, fear, position and limited knowledge, without claiming both are objective fact.
9. **Delayed consequence:** Damage, territory, surviving enemies, debt, reputations and relationships can rebound later from events that actually occurred.
10. **Ordinary thing becomes ominous:** A recurring meal, object or habit can be reinterpreted through new context; e.g., approved **BONELESS LASAGNA** occurs in both Chris's apparent B.2 disaster and waking reality. The dream status must remain concealed until Chris awakens, and only the dream contains his RDR2/PSN destruction and attempted Kancho.

### Action and tactical reversals
11. **Opponent learns:** A survivor can adapt to a previously effective Sin tactic with causal knowledge and limits; do not nerf a character arbitrarily.
12. **Victory sabotages objective:** Winning a fight can damage the objective, lose an informant or invite retaliation; preserve costs and causality.
13. **Underestimated actor matters:** Established abilities, experience or senses (for example Arthur, or Chris) enable a consequential choice, not sudden new powers.
14. **Genre-shifting interruption:** Credible danger may interrupt comedy, or earned comedy follow terror, without changing character fidelity or granting impossible knowledge.
15. **Enemy refuses expected fight:** Informed opponents may bargain, retreat, deceive, flee, split up, call aid or choose a different battlefield; preserve agency.

### Character, comedy, and long-term payoff
16. **Joke becomes evidence:** A previously thrown-away remark/habit can later expose a real lie or omission, fairly seeded and retrospectively legible.
17. **Accidentally useful employee:** Charles's rambling inmate interview report may include legitimate intelligence that Chris finally verifies; Charles's Pepsi/peanut-butter compensation demands remain character-driven. This is a *possible* beat, not a completed report or guaranteed result.
18. **Relationship reversal:** Habitual antagonists can cooperate effectively in a real crisis, with earned motives, then retain their disagreements afterward.
19. **Misunderstood loyalty:** A seemingly selfish, cruel or disloyal act may have another demonstrably supported motive; consequences are not undone by the reveal.
20. **Callback with altered emotional meaning:** Early humor, possessions or routines may return with frightening or tragic meaning through real character history, not empty repeated references.

## C. Three author-approved creative priorities
- **Consequence-driven reversals:** Wins and defeats produce tangible follow-on problems, adaptation, injury, loot changes, debt, territory, captives, reputation and retaliatory intent.
- **Character-driven deception:** Walter White, Agent 47, Charles Manson, Vecna, the Seven Sins, and any relevant character can misrepresent or withhold information according to actual motives and access. Never treat lies as objective narrator facts.
- **Tonal contrast:** Serious psychological horror can coexist with ordinary absurdity (Chris's Pepsi, RDR2, Charles's warden negotiations) when viewpoint, timing, location and information access are legitimate; neither tone trivializes the other's real consequences.

## D. Mandatory twist / storytelling QA, when a twist is used
1. **Earned?** The mechanism follows on-page causes, known abilities, and fair clues.
2. **Concealed fairly?** Do not spoil, contradict objective facts, invent knowledge, or deceptively relocate characters; a subjective dream/perception can be presented as real only when later honestly reconciled.
3. **Consequential?** Revelation alters choices, risk, relationships, access, meaning, danger or other persistent outcomes, not only a momentary shock.
4. **TTS comprehensible?** Listeners can follow who speaks, what is happening, where the action is, and ultimately what was real. Conceal only mystery-bearing interpretation, never basic auditory orientation.

**No twist quota.** Use straightforward confrontations when stronger. **No authorial omniscience breaches, free equipment, fake irreversible consequences, resets, unearned escapes or mandatory cast stupidity.** Established physical/supernatural limits and regional/source ownership still control actual mechanics.

## E. Source ownership, precedence, and state
This additive author's approval **supersedes the prior '20 new storytelling ideas remain proposals' status** in the global pacing correction, elevating **all 20 to approved optional development techniques**. It does not elevate hypothetical dialogue, sample encounters, or proposed character successes to **played facts**. Retain latest author-approved global pace, B.2 concealed nightmare and overlap, Billy first-person special narration and main-story narrator limits. Future integration routes to Season 3 presentation/narration and outline owners via R9 index/graph, not history archives or COMPLETE route-only views. Do not overwrite nine controlling bundles or claim live installation without verified cutover.

**Checkpoint:** Season 3 main delivered PROVISIONAL through IV.3, D215/R67; B.1 ALOYSIUS separately PROVISIONAL, revised replay HELD; B.2 THE FIRST DEVOUR next UNWRITTEN on explicit PLAY only. No story time advancement, no new episodes delivered.


## Embedded author approval: KAZE_SEASON3_NATURAL_LENGTH_AND_AUDIENCE_MISDIRECTION_AUTHOR_LOCK_20261010.md

# THE SINS / R9 — AUTHOR LOCK: EARNED LENGTH AND FAIR AUDIENCE MISDIRECTION

**Date:** 2026-10-10. **Authority:** Latest explicit author correction > earlier recommendations and development proposals. **Status:** Author-approved future editorial and presentation rules, locally recorded for safe later integration. Not a played scene, not an approved replacement of existing episode cards, not a live project installation. **NO PLAY.**

## A. Billy prehistoric specials: organically earned episode count and runtime

- Billy gets **exactly as many episodes as the material naturally earns**. Do NOT aim for 4, 10, 12 or any other preselected total as a content-length goal. Earlier assistant recommendation of 10–12 is **superseded as a target**. The original **B.1–B.4 legacy cards remain currently routed pending an actual approved expanded outline**, and the author has approved expansion in principle, with **no fixed final count yet**.
- Outline the dramatic, thematic and causal developments first; let compelling episode breaks, transitions and sustained conflicts determine count and runtime second. Do not milk scenes, prolong minor jokes, stretch encounters, insert arbitrary three-act setbacks, or turn source idea banks into quotas.
- Conversely do not squeeze a developed and consequential arc into a short scene solely for a preset runtime. A beat may be brief, a centerpiece may take substantial time: its value depends on momentum, evolving stakes, character agency, surprise, action and consequences.
- Distinguish author-locked canonical facts and selected outcomes from 30/60/15 approved *development ideas*; approval authorizes use and shaping, not automatic execution of every proposal. Preserve Mama's protective motive final reveal, first devour, killer reveal/hat provenance, and selected B.4 Brusta swallow, at corresponding dramatic locations in any future reoutline.
- Complete verbatim author-approved Candidate C **MORE'N YOU KNOW** lyrics only when dramatizing an actual full performance; Version A is historical/rollback-only. Other callbacks may mention, hum or play the tune without restating lyrics. Do not abbreviate, blend versions or rewrite a lyrical performance.

## B. Chris / Charles B.2 nightmare: conceal the medium until the wake-up

**Critical audience-fidelity lock:** In the B.2 Chris/Charles PS9/RDR2 disaster scene, **DO NOT disclose or signal in narration, headings, labels, introductory framing, commentary, or character dialogue that it is a dream/nightmare before Chris actually awakens.** Stage the initial events as real to Chris and as apparently real to the audience. Let the impossibly catastrophic deletions, Chris's justified panic, his full author-locked rant, and the attempted *Pacific Rim Kancho* unfold with escalating stakes. **Chris STARTLES AWAKE BEFORE IMPACT.** Only **at the actual wake-up** can the audience learn the preceding events were his nightmare.

- The audience should genuinely be tricked on first encounter, but not cheated: the subsequent reveal reconciles every physical fact; dream-only RDR2 saves, PlayStation Network deletion, Charles's attempted cult scheme, shutdown/concealment, damage, and Kancho did NOT occur in awake continuity. Venlee's >90%-complete Assassin's Creed IV: Black Flag deletion remains real past history. The awake RDR2/PSN account is intact, and awake Charles is making **BONELESS LASAGNA**. Preserve the exact author-written nightmare rant and awake line from `topic_sources/Billy_B2_Charles_Nightmare_20261009.txt`.
- Avoid conspicuous clue-signposting such as 'unbeknownst to him', 'it felt strangely unreal', 'none of this was really happening', dreamlike glowing transitions, a conspicuous scene heading marked DREAM, an external narrator diagnosing the dream, or premature reality-status qualifiers. Natural details that reward hindsight are fine if not giveaway labels.
- The audience can be misled; **the underlying continuity ledger cannot**. Record events as subjective dream content internally, not real deaths, destroyed saves, lost loot, broken fixtures, physical contact, elapsed story days or acquired capabilities. No false new character omniscience: exterior Sins and awake Charles cannot know Chris's unshared dream. Chris may report it afterward, subject to actual access and his choice.
- Preserve the exclusive **Billy first-person narrator** rule for prehistoric specials. Do not solve the twist by installing Mimir or a god's-eye narrator, or by pretending Billy sees into Chris's sleeping head. Use an audience-facing subjective presentation that can be understood through Chris's own experience, without attributing knowledge to Billy; Billy's first-person framing can resume or explain only what he legitimately learns after Chris wakes/reports. Exact staging remains open; it must pass both the spoiler-concealment test and the viewpoint/access test.
- The reveal must come after a meaningful apparent catastrophe, not several episodes later at the cost of confusing what was actual. Keep B.2's real childhood survival/first devour as its primary spine. This is one earned twist, not a license for arbitrary fakeouts.

## C. Global Season 3 storytelling toolbox: earned craft, not a formula

The author explicitly extends the audience-misdirection principle and greater range of story tactics to **all of Season 3**, including ordinary main episodes (whose usual narrator remains Black Mimir under existing first-person and bounded exception rules) and Billy specials (Billy exclusive first-person narrator).

**Use the most contextually effective devices, when earned:**
1. **Fair surprise / concealed premise:** Hide a vital interpretation until the revealing action, without lying about external facts or giving impossible narrator knowledge.
2. **Delayed revelation:** Let a consequence, identity, motive or cause be discovered after its visible effects when characters would plausibly not know it yet.
3. **Dramatic irony:** Audience and character sometimes know different things; exploit the gap without arbitrary omniscience.
4. **Character-limited viewpoint / unreliable testimony:** Let mistaken, self-serving, lying or traumatized speakers misinterpret evidence; their *claims* aren't objective authorial truth.
5. **Reversals and recontextualization:** A legitimate choice or discovery changes what a prior scene meant, rather than a deus-ex-machina reset.
6. **Red herrings with proper causal alternatives:** Plausible misleading evidence can prompt genuine investigation and agency, but clues and answers must remain reconcilable.
7. **Setup/payoff and escalating callbacks:** Reuse a minor trait/object/joke for growing stakes or meaning, not for empty repetition.
8. **Cross-cutting and contrast:** Intercut serious danger with lived gang humor only where locations, communication and actual knowledge permit; don't use psychic scene cuts as information transfers.
9. **Withheld offscreen information:** Reveal what someone did later through traceable evidence, consequences or firsthand testimony; don't skip the meaningful conflict just to hide a result.
10. **Horror through implication / restraint:** A silence, reaction, omitted explanation or small action can be more potent than melodramatic narration.
11. **Organic character contradiction:** Let habits, loyalties, biases, moral ambiguity, humor and self-defeating choices create surprising conduct with genuine consequences.
12. **Escalation / payoff inversion:** Allow the apparent comic or trivial detail to become consequential, or frightening danger to create character-specific comedy, without making participants stupid.

**Hard limits:** No twist quota per episode, no constant fake death, no fake consequence erased to force thrills, no artificial cliffhanger, no retroactive narrator lie represented as objective truth, no unearned secret knowledge, no involuntary character stupidity, no unauthorized teleport/telepathy/power/escape. Preserve TTS listener orientation about what is observable (speaker, place, timing, relevant actions) while selectively withholding only the mystery-bearing *truth*. Ambiguity should intrigue, not make the scene impossible to follow. Real wounds, loot, prisoners, debts, loyalties and territorial changes persist; dreams/false statements are explicitly tagged in internal continuity after their eventual reveal, not turned into played external fact.

**Editing acceptance test:** If the reveal is disclosed before Chris wakes, FAIL. If the surprise requires Billy to know thoughts he never learned, FAIL. If the viewer cannot understand what physically happened after the reveal, FAIL. If a scene exists only to inflate special count, FAIL. If the device grows organically out of character, evidence and causality and does not spoil its own reveal, PASS.

### Illness, possessions and private information

Track **biological infection / perceived symptoms / named diagnosis / belief about prognosis** separately. The accepted late-Chapter-3 Arthur has latent pre-diagnosis TB, not his later exhausted condition. A cough does not itself tell him the diagnosis. Chris's knowledge and anxiety are Chris's, not Arthur's. The later diagnosis scene confirms TB; game health-core rules and consumable penalties remain interface mechanics, not a new medical law of the crossover.

Arthur's actual extraction equipment must be individually sourced: weapons, ammunition, horse, tack, money, satchel contents, clothing, customization and condition are not generated from what the player *could* own. Preserve known ability and habits without importing an endgame inventory. The private-information ledger separately tracks journal pages/drawings, letters, personal memories, conversations and who can access them. The game exposing one private entry does not expose every memory or make another character omniscient.

### Fixed-record and progress contract

Chris's game is a fixed representation of original RDR2 history/narrative. Living Arthur's later behavior cannot rewrite it. Ordinary save/reload, player choices and game branches still work as software. A changed game choice is not a changed prediction of current reality.

After Chris's protected save is eventually deleted by an **unresolved cause**, he eventually allows Arthur to play from the opening. Arthur learns progressively, can improve with a controller, and completes Chapters 1–6 and both epilogues. Playing never grants Arthur's skill to Chris. Training remains the separate route for Chris's earned gunfighting/Dead Eye. Playback progress begins **null** at the KNOCKTURNAL pilot.

Record only after legitimate exposure: accepted event; player/viewer; chapter/mission/checkpoint; optional/honor/choice flags; exact scene seen or spoiler heard; source and recipients; lived-memory comparison; new knowledge versus uncertain inference; response and later decisions. Save deletion/reload cannot erase a viewer's already-formed memory. Future game knowledge persists but is not infallible prophecy or a command to act.

## WALTER WHITE / JESSE PINKMAN — s5 CHECKLIST, SNAPSHOT AND FIDELITY GATE

This is the explicitly accepted s5 scope expansion, not a performed scene.

**Walter White / Heisenberg** and **Jesse Pinkman** are accepted on Billy's future acquisition/checklist and the KNOCKTURNAL imported-roster registry. Their existence on the checklist does not guarantee an encounter, swallow, capture, cooperation or first-attempt success.

### Snapshot lock

Use **Breaking Bad — immediately after Season 4 Episode 13, `Face Off`, before Jesse learns that Walt poisoned Brock**.

This snapshot is chosen to preserve:

- fully developed late-Season-4 Heisenberg behavior/capability;
- a highly competent Jesse;
- damaged but still operational Walt/Jesse cooperation;
- Brock poisoning as a still-hidden relationship bomb rather than already-resolved open hatred.

### Fidelity gate — COMPLETED 2026-09-21

Status: **COMPLETE TO THE PROTECTED-UNKNOWN BOUNDARY.** Walter and Jesse are now authoring-ready at the locked snapshot. This is production research only: it grants neither character new in-world knowledge, does not instantiate their arrival, and does not resolve the causal acquisition/cooperation/serum unknowns below.

Evidence basis used for this pass: AMC's official *Breaking Bad* series/episode material; AMC's Aaron Paul retrospective on Jesse's Season-4 scenes; the verified **Breaking Bad & Better Call Saul** official channel's *Salud* and *Confessions* clips; the selected *Face Off* episode dialogue/subtitle record; the immediately preceding *End Times*, *Salud* and *Crawl Space* continuity; and *Face Off* promotional/episode stills for appearance. Primary/official material controls where available; transcript/still corroboration fills snapshot detail without importing later knowledge.

#### Walter White / Heisenberg — locked late-S4 fidelity

**CONFIRMED — appearance at the selected endpoint**
- Bald head; wire-frame glasses; close goatee/mustache; lean middle-aged build.
- In the final *Face Off* hospital-parking-garage exchange he is in the bright green long-sleeve button shirt and dark trousers used through the finale. Residual late-S4 facial injury is still part of the immediate continuity from the Walt/Jesse fight; do not cosmetically reset him to an untouched earlier-season appearance.
- S34 appearance-only override permits the iconic black pork-pie hat and dark classic Heisenberg clothing/jacket after naturally obtaining/assembling them in-world. Bald head, wire-frame glasses, close goatee/mustache, controlled posture and intimidating stillness complete that presentation. No later Season-5 memories, events, empire resources, cash, associates, injuries, knowledge or status are imported. Existing actual injuries evolve only through supported continuity.

**CONFIRMED — competence and limits**
- Walter is an exceptional chemist and elite methamphetamine cook whose chemical reasoning, synthesis discipline, improvised problem-solving, contamination awareness and process control are central demonstrated competencies.
- At this point he has also repeatedly applied chemistry/engineering knowledge to improvised criminal problems, including poison and explosive planning, but those feats remain task-specific rather than proof of universal science mastery.
- No source at this snapshot establishes Walter as a geneticist, virologist, surgeon, ocular-neurosurgery specialist, Extremis authority or turnkey biomedical-serum designer. Those domains remain outside his automatic expertise.

**CONFIRMED — psychology / control style**
- Pride, ego, need for control and refusal to accept subordinate powerlessness are mature late-S4 drivers. Walter increasingly tries to define the available choices for other people instead of merely persuading them.
- Manipulation is often indirect: with Jesse he exploits fear, attachment, incomplete information and time pressure, then presents the conclusion he wants as the rational/necessary one. The Brock scheme is the decisive snapshot proof: Walter secretly poisons Brock with lily of the valley and redirects Jesse's suspicion toward Gus.
- He can move rapidly from pleading or apparently reasonable explanation into cold certainty, threat, intimidation or lethal planning when control is threatened. He is still capable of panic, improvisation and visible fear; late-S4 Walter is not omniscient or permanently composed.

**CONFIRMED — Walter vs. Heisenberg presentation**
- Walter White and Heisenberg are **one person**, not two consciousnesses and not a dissociative identity. “Heisenberg” is Walter's cultivated criminal identity/presentation: firmer posture, greater menace, controlled certainty, status assertion and willingness to use fear.
- Ordinary Walter presentation can be softer, professorial, defensive, familial, anxious or pleading. He can switch presentation strategically, but the same memory, motives and responsibility remain continuous across both modes.

**CONFIRMED — voice / dialogue generation**
- Default late-S4 Walter: controlled, precise, explanatory and corrective; often uses full sentences, technical or causal framing, pointed rhetorical questions, repetition and a teacher-like insistence that the listener follow his reasoning.
- Under pressure he can become faster, sharper and more repetitive; intimidation is often quieter and more declarative rather than constant shouting.
- Profanity is available but not a verbal tic. Do not write him as continuously swearing or as a one-note growling “Heisenberg.”
- With Jesse, “Jesse” itself is frequently used to regain attention/control, alternating between pleading, correction, emotional leverage and command.

**CONFIRMED — knowledge / resources / relationships at the exact snapshot**
- Gus Fring, Hector Salamanca and Tyrus are dead; Walter knows his plan caused Gus's death. He and Jesse have destroyed the superlab.
- Walter knows Brock was poisoned by **his own** lily-of-the-valley scheme and knows Jesse still does not know that truth. He also knows Jesse's ricin suspicion was manipulated away from him.
- Walter knows Jesse has learned that Brock was *not* poisoned by ricin. Jesse has accepted, for the moment, Walter's position that Gus still had to die.
- Walter retains access to Saul Goodman as his criminal lawyer/fixer connection, his own vehicle/home and the surviving personal/criminal resources not lost in the prior crisis; the superlab and Gus's command structure are no longer usable infrastructure.
- His family relationship is severely strained and fear-contaminated; Skyler's immediate finale reaction is wary enough to ask whether Walt caused the explosion, and Walter answers from a position of triumph.
- Relationship with Jesse: temporary operational reconciliation after severe rupture, not restored trust. Walter is carrying a major hidden betrayal that Jesse has not discovered.

**UNKNOWN / DO NOT INVENT**
- Exact future cash, equipment and logistical inventory at the instant of Elsewhering unless the event route establishes what comes with him.
- Any biomedical knowledge, materials or post-*Face Off* learning not yet earned.
- Any later Season-5 status, empire resources, associates, injuries or knowledge.

#### Jesse Pinkman — locked late-S4 fidelity

**CONFIRMED — appearance at the selected endpoint**
- Closely buzzed hair; short facial stubble/goatee; lean build; visible forearm/hand tattoos.
- In the final *Face Off* parking-garage scene he wears a plain black short-sleeve T-shirt with dark pants.
- He carries the immediate physical continuity of the late-S4 Walt/Jesse fight and Mexico period; do not import later captivity scars or *El Camino* appearance.

**CONFIRMED — practical lab/cook competence**
- Jesse is no longer merely an inexperienced street cook. In *Salud* he takes control of a hostile cartel lab, enforces cleanliness/process requirements, performs the cook under scrutiny and produces a reported **96.2% purity** batch. Gus subsequently treats him as capable of running the superlab without Walt.
- His competence is heavily practical/procedural and learned through repeated work with Walter and the superlab. He can execute the established meth process, notice contamination/process problems and run a lab routine under pressure.
- This does not make Jesse Walter's equal in broad theoretical chemistry, nor does it grant automatic expertise in unfamiliar biomedical, genetic, virological, surgical or ocular systems.

**CONFIRMED — psychology at the exact snapshot**
- Jesse is carrying severe guilt and trauma from killing Gale and from the escalating violence around him. AMC's Aaron Paul retrospective specifically identifies his Season-4 “Problem Dog” confession as a partial attempt to confess that guilt.
- He retains strong empathy and attachment, especially around children. Brock's poisoning produces immediate terror, rage and a willingness to point a gun at Walter when he believes Walt is responsible.
- He can become explosively angry when he believes someone has exploited or harmed people he cares about, but he is not mindlessly volatile: he listens to evidence, changes conclusions and can act decisively under pressure.
- By late S4 his self-respect and competence have increased. In Mexico he can confront senior lab personnel, give orders and demand standards instead of automatically deferring.

**CONFIRMED — Walt attachment / distrust structure**
- Jesse's bond with Walter is damaged, ambivalent and still powerful. Immediately before the finale Jesse had rejected Walter's pleas and resented his manipulation and cruelty, yet he also explicitly refused to let Gus kill Walter.
- In *End Times* Jesse arrives ready to kill Walter over Brock; Walter talks him out of it by redirecting suspicion toward Gus. At the end of *Face Off*, after learning Brock was not poisoned by ricin, Jesse questions the logic but accepts that Gus still had to die and shakes Walter's hand.
- Therefore the snapshot is **not** “best friends restored.” It is an unstable operational truce built on incomplete information, old attachment and fresh manipulation.

**CONFIRMED — voice / dialogue generation**
- Jesse speaks informally, often in compressed or fragmented sentences, with fillers such as “man,” “yo,” “dude,” and profanity when emotionally or socially natural. “Bitch” is part of his vocabulary but is not punctuation for every sentence.
- Under stress he repeats accusations/questions, interrupts, jumps between emotional and practical points and can speak very quickly.
- When focused on a task he becomes much more direct and operational; the cartel-lab sequence shows him issuing concrete procedural orders rather than talking like a meme version of himself.
- Do not over-intellectualize his diction, but do not write him as stupid. His practical competence, street awareness and emotional perception are real.

**CONFIRMED — knowledge / resources / relationships at the exact snapshot**
- Jesse knows Gus is dead, the superlab has been destroyed, Brock is expected to recover, and toxicology showed **lily of the valley rather than ricin**.
- Jesse does **not** know Walter poisoned Brock and does **not** yet know Huell/Saul removed the ricin cigarette as part of Walter's manipulation. That revelation occurs much later and is excluded by the snapshot lock.
- Jesse remains strongly attached to Andrea and Brock and returns toward them after the garage conversation.
- Mike is not an immediately available partner at this exact point; he was left recovering in Mexico after the cartel operation. Gus's organization has just been decapitated.
- Jesse retains his home/vehicle and whatever ordinary personal possessions are causally present, but exact arrival inventory must be established by the displacement route rather than assumed wholesale.
- Relationship with Walter is temporary cooperation plus unresolved dependence, attachment and distrust; the hidden Brock betrayal is capable of catastrophically changing that relationship if later discovered through legitimate evidence.

**UNKNOWN / DO NOT INVENT**
- Exact items, cash, weapons or chemicals physically carried at the moment of displacement unless the arrival scene establishes them.
- Any knowledge from Season 5, including the truth of Walt's Brock poisoning, later ricin reconstruction, later empire events or captivity.
- Any automatic biomedical/serum competence beyond Jesse's demonstrated practical lab/process ability and whatever he is legitimately taught later.

### Authoring lock after research

Walter and Jesse may now receive meaningful portrayal when causal event access legitimately activates them. Their portrayal must remain inside this post-*Face Off* snapshot and the fields above. The research pass does **not** schedule their appearance, make them allies, guarantee Billy can swallow them, grant either one knowledge of KAZE systems, or resolve whether they cooperate with Chris.

### Serum role

Walter eventually materially helps Chris create **VIOLET ASCENDANCY SERUM**, whose three-branch mechanics and Singed collaboration are owned by SRC-01. Preserve expertise boundaries:

Walter is an exceptional chemist, **not automatically** a geneticist, virologist, surgeon, Extremis expert or ocular-neurosurgery expert. His legitimate contribution can include:

- process chemistry;
- synthesis;
- purification;
- analytical troubleshooting;
- contamination control;
- reaction conditions;
- scaling;
- improvised lab/equipment solutions;
- chemical stability/manufacturing once sufficient real biomedical/Extremis/ocular information and source material exist.

Jesse can contribute materially to hands-on process execution, repetition, practical lab discipline and manufacturing once properly taught/provided the necessary information.

The project must still establish legitimate biomedical source knowledge/materials. Walter may not magically derive the entire serum from chemistry intuition alone.

Exact Billy route to Walter/Jesse, swallow circumstances, cooperation/coercion/bargain, exact implementation of the committed future Singed biomedical collaboration, source of Extremis material/research, source of Earth-1610 ocular augmentation knowledge/material, serum testing, and administration remain causal/protected unless explicitly resolved later.

## S29 APPROVED ROSTER / FIDELITY COMMIT
User approved the researched active-version policy, then explicitly committed it with esketit. These exact identity/source-point selections replace older unresolved or conflicting bindings for these same slots; no new physical person is created by a version correction. This is project configuration, not a claim that every selected game ending coexists in one franchise timeline.

The 63 named Wii Armageddon slots include Khameleon, exclude Kreate-a-Fighter and reuse the four existing Scorpion/Goro/Drahmin/Meat rows. Cyrax/Sektor stay MK11 cyborgs. Scorpion is Hanzo, Sub-Zero is Kuai Liang, Noob is Bi-Han. Classic Dairou and Havik remain separate. MK1 General Shao occupies the Shao Kahn roster slot. No Titan/Blaze/Hourglass victory rewards are granted from hypothetical endings. Story-supported compatible endings may define a selected point only as explicitly listed.

Each short profile is a bounded orientation reference. Retrieve the selected game's actual dialogue/technique evidence for any unresolved consequential detail; speech is not a bag of catchphrases. Full audio audition, every frame of carried gear, all optional lines and every original comic panel were not verified. Gameplay bars, turns, Fatality prompts, rounds and boss health are not literal world physics. A known move is not an unavoidable hit. Sparse Kameo/classic appearances stay sparse; no invented biography, training or exact offscreen departure scene.

**Identity collisions:** New Era Kenshi's Jax is not automatically this roster's mature MK11 Jax; Raiden's Kuai-Scorpion mentor is not Hanzo; Smoke's child Hanzo is not the adult Hanzo slot; Bi-Han's female armored Sektor is not the robot Sektor slot. Source-history memories do not fuse counterparts. Living Sindel and Jerrod-led Ermac are selected before her absorption, preventing a second Sindel soul. Teammates, mounts, armies and referenced places are not automatic additional admissions or imported geography.

**Authority:** user identity corrections > approved selected source point > demonstrated source-consistent behavior/mechanics > incidental art/gameplay abstraction. No current location, target knowledge, prisoner or encounter follows from admission. Existing world and independent-pressure rules still apply.

### S29 ADDITIONAL FIDELITY / RESEARCH LIMITS

**Billy_Tahm** — Text speech pass closed: cultivated charm, appetite/river/bargain metaphors, personalized insults, vanity and menace. User profanity and absurd humor are custom additions. No imported contract mechanics, forced comic relief or combat-delaying monologues.

**Arthur** — Low-honor text research closed at bounded level. Distinguish honor variants from elective antagonize dialogue and separate final help-John/return-for-money choices. No mandatory indiscriminate aggression or inability to show loyalty. Preserve custom cybernetic body and existing project memories; do not replay game death.

**Obito** — Preserve explicit manga ch563/564 adult white-mask pre-contact binding. No later Ten-Tails, redemption or afterlife knowledge; research outline is not a substitute for exact chapter mechanics when consequential.

**Thor_GOW** — Ragnarök Asgard battle while fighting the younger Jörmungandr; physical violence, shame, trauma and family concern, before his later refusal of Odin. Current joint displacement brings BOTH into Noxian coastal waters in XVI.1 D120, with a closed rupture and separate ensuing agency. No MCU traits or later memories. Other imports retain their individual timing.

**Jormungandr_GOW** — Ragnarok battle serpent and source-specific time rupture; not automatically the older Lake of Nine incarnation with all its later memories. Ancient language is not omnilingual human chatter. Entry remains future and self-causal.

**John_1911** — RDR1 final-defense dialogue supports terse protective father, dry humor and practical response; project-authorized post-death viable awakening retained. No 1914 future knowledge, no merger with RDR2 group John.

**Jack_1914** — After Remember My Family as already selected. Polite deception while seeking Ross becomes explicit grievance and resolved revenge. No pending Ross hunt, no canon death for Ross’s relatives or invented later career.

Research routes: Tahm developer interview https://www.surrenderat20.net/2015/06/red-post-collection-champion-insights.html and recorded lines https://www.nerfplz.com/lol-champion-quotes/tahm-kench/ ; Arthur dialogue https://en.wikiquote.org/wiki/Red_Dead_Redemption_2 ; Obito official retrospective https://naruto-official.com/en/news/01_1706 ; God of War transcript https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Gorzkun/God-of-War-Script-Analysis/main/Script_God_Of_War_Ragnarok.txt ; RDR1 final missions https://wikiwiki.jp/rdr/THE%20LAST%20ENEMY%20THAT%20SHALL%20BE%20DESTROYED and https://wikiwiki.jp/rdr/Remember%20My%20Family .

These researched guardrails do not overwrite already-fixed source points. Protected King identity, Herobrine ontology, Scuba Steve's diegetic status, ordinary-guy Jesus identity, Peter Cullen actor-versus-character distinction and unprovided current Arcane micro-timings remain protected; s33 explicitly resolves the named opening phase pins. The reboot earlier-history Hulk encounter is classic Savage Hulk (#181) by explicit user selection; unselected old Doom endpoint remains historical/unknown. Local source repair cannot manufacture those missing records. Original comic-panel and exact-loadout gaps remain for the selected Venompool/Venom-Punisher and parts of Headpool evidence; supported identity choices are committed while dependent facts remain gated.

## S32 LIGHT / OMEGA TEEMO FIDELITY ROUTING

**Light Yagami:** retain the already selected post-Higuchi/Yotsuba-memory-return manga snapshot as experienced Kira. Current branch evidence says Venn already proved notebook function; do not revert portrayal to beginner proof-testing or heart-attack-only caution.

**Omega Squad Teemo future transition:** this is a project-specific future transformation triggered by the committed mask-binding event, not current source-state replacement before that event. When triggered, portrayal uses Omega Squad Teemo's dedicated Riot voice/appearance corpus: terse war-survivor mentality, attrition/reinforcement pessimism, dark humor, attachment avoidance, self-aware ugly history, dirty-fighting pragmatism and scout identity. Do not infer generic military biography beyond the committed Omega memory/competence package or turn him into an AI. The project explicitly grants both-histories-as-lived memory and Omega competence at the transition; those are branch mechanics, not claims about base Riot continuity.

## S33 reboot imported fidelity — current scoped pins
Daredevil and Bullseye retain the project-selected Born Again Season 2 Episode 7 pins below; no later finale/CIA knowledge. Daredevil uses sensory evidence, law/investigation, Catholic guilt, street protection and moral resistance to killing; desperate cooperation with dangerous allies need not make him an Enforcer subordinate. His earlier faceoffs with Kaze and Daniel are distinct, caused encounters; never invent physical parity with Kaze.
Bullseye is physically extraordinary in projectile geometry/accuracy, not magical/omniscient. He is unstable/self-directed; Silco reputation -> verification -> compartmentalized trial -> further contracts only while interests align. Not permanent obedience.

AMBESSA'S PERSONAL OPERATION: BREAK THEIR HOME AND PUT PRICES ON THEIR FAMILY
Use the gang’s established EXTERIOR workshop/store, not the interior workshop shown in the new BELLY sketch or an invented beloved childhood home. KAZE KILLS VIKTOR; THE NETWORK REACTS
The first bodily death occurs in XIV.3's retaliatory confrontation. It is a real killing of that body, not a pretended miss or a reveal that Kaze pulled the blow. Use one selected version and source point per participant. Do not combine incompatible films, games, future upgrades or an older incarnation's later memories because their names match. DARKIN CROSS-WEAPON CONTACT / FUSION EVIDENCE RULE — LATEST AUTHOR QUESTION (7 OCT 2026)
Two distinct phenomena: (1) holding/carrying/wielding a second Darkin weapon is physically plausible, subject to size/access, and (2) merging sentient essence, taking independent control or combining granted powers is NOT a universal established Darkin property. Riot's The Legend of the Darkin describes imprisoning individual souls within their carried weapons; official Kayn profile says Kayn struggles for control with Rhaast inside the scythe. Legends of Runeterra's alternate Rhaast outcome artwork/flavor depicts Rhaast acquiring Xolaani's Bloodletters; the artwork does not certify a lasting fusion or inherited Xolaani abilities in the project's active Runeterra timeline. Xolaani's blood-magic domination and exceptional forms belong to her documented abilities, not all Darkin. No default shared hive mind, free power stacks, two souls disappearing into one, invincible mega-Darkin, resurrection or handwaved host. A hypothetical fusion would require independently depicted ritual/mechanic, participant resistance/consent as relevant, cost, resulting anatomy, preserved identity and its own explicit authorial world-rule choice; it is NOT approved simply by asking whether possible. Kaze's identity-stripped Black Marrow and old-branch Aatrox remain separate special cases.
SOURCES: https://universe.leagueoflegends.com/en_US/story/the-legend-of-the-darkin/ ; https://www.leagueoflegends.com/en-us/champions/kayn/ ; LoR alternate Rhaast card/text is conditional scenario, not fixed game-world history.

DARKIN — CURRENT RUNETERRA NATIVE ENSEMBLE / STATUS AND DEPLOYMENT OWNER
CURRENT AUTHOR SELECTION: The 23 retained named Darkin belong to the selected historical/potential Runeterra world cast. They may matter through authentic history, weapon prisons, supported carriers/hosts or legitimately extant independent beings. Varus is excluded from story deployment; only historical source references to him remain. This is a cast/world-selection decision ONLY. It does not deliver a new episode, place a character at an unspecified scene, unblock a weapon, create a body/host, promote them into the fixed 37-identity S3 operative cast, revive an ancient casualty, or require 23 appearances inside the remaining S3 cards. This is not a new faction that universally cooperates.

SOURCE PRIORITY AND CHRONOLOGY
Riot’s The Legend of the Darkin and Twilight of the Gods give the ancient war and imprisonment background; Riot champion biographies own individually contemporary material. Legends of Runeterra item texts and the Darkin Saga supply names/weapons/possible depicted situations, but game-card manifestation and contradictory alternate climaxes are NOT automatically delivered branch history. The source-world present is never automatically a project-current position or informed relationship. Present-day whereabouts/custody remain UNKNOWN until actually observed/received. Aatrox’s Kaze-specific prior erased-branch history and Kaze’s carried abilities are separate from any legitimately reestablished current-branch Aatrox.

SITUATION MODEL
• Captive weapon: an intelligent consciousness bound into an existing weapon; identification, location, carrier, consent and resistance remain real. Possession is not inevitable simply because a weapon is seen or handled; exact contact/host rules follow the specific Darkin.
• Manifest: formed body through source-supported vessel, blood/flesh transformation, pack, spirit/tree exception or legitimate branch event. That body is not automatically immortal, invulnerable or an unrestricted teleport interface. Destroying an external body does not by itself establish complete destruction of a bound-weapon consciousness.
• Unknown survivor: no assigned prison, weapon, host, country or death. Historical evidence alone cannot place them next to the gang.
• Ancient dead: historical person, testimony, monuments, artifacts or remembered consequences ONLY. No restored individual without a separate explicit author decision and a legitimate changed chronology.
• Distinguish actor knowledge: Kaze does not gain every Darkin’s identity, location or secrets merely from his own permanent Darkin nature; Black Marrow’s old Aatrox essence is stripped of Aatrox’s identity. No universal Darkin sensing, telepathy, automatic obedience, shared memories, resurrection, collective alert or instant war summons.

NARRATIVE ROUTES
History around Shurima, Targon, Bandle City, Freljord, Zaun, Bilgewater/Serpent Isles, Ionia/Spirit Realm and the Shadow Isles can yield real clues or costly discoveries. Include history only when someone has a source, stake and actual access; regions do not collapse into the Noxian capital. Act III–V Black Rose/Briar/Zaahen/Kaze intersections are possible rather than mandatory scenes: darkin blood-magic scholarship may affect an investigation without proving that Briar contains Darkin blood or that a Darkin weapon is automatically awakened. Darkin have conflicting appetites and philosophies, notably Aatrox’s oblivion, Rhaast’s predation, Xolaani’s coercion, Naafiri’s pack supremacy and Zaahen’s resistance; do not turn them into a single obedient Darkin army or write a revival to meet a fight quota.

CONNECTION TO THE SINS
Kaze’s retained Darkin nature and The Black Marrow are independent of an external possessed Darkin blade; completed Kaze/Aatrox assimilation is historical, not a fresh automatic buff, current Aatrox clone, or mutual recognition channel. The Kaze/Kayn-Rhaast and Kaze/Zaahen one-on-one selections remain PROSPECTIVE and must obtain an actual entry/motive; no other named Darkin gets a free forced duel. Billy’s storage and prison, ordinary cargo, witness movement and rumor routes obey their own ownership and access firewalls. Kaze has no obligation to turn these entities into a new team or to collect them all.

BRIAR SELECTED FUTURE ADMISSION, NOT CURRENTLY INDUCTED
Briar is ALREADY among the 12 selected Noxian-native identities; she is NOT a Darkin. The author now explicitly selects her eventual voluntary Seven Sins membership as a future story destination. She is not adopted as a child, controlled as equipment, instantly healed of frenzy or pressed into a vacancy left by Frank. No contact or admission is played yet at D173. Frank’s distinct voluntary departure is selected for a separately earned action and does not automatically remove his existing D173 membership.

NAMED INDIVIDUALS AND COMPATIBILITY STATUS (23; VARUS EXCLUDED)
01. Aatrox — SWORD_HOST_MANIFESTATION_WITH_BRANCH_RESET_CONFLICT. Established weapon category: greatsword. Former Shuriman defender seeking oblivion through mass destruction. Original Kaze branch: Kaze defeated him and erased his identity; whether the Second Temporal Relaunch restored an independent present-branch Aatrox remains unresolved. Do not re-create him from identity-stripped essence in Black Marrow.
02. Rhaast — SCYTHE_CONTESTED_BY_KAYN. Established weapon category: scythe. The living Darkin in Kayn’s weapon seeks to control Kayn’s body; no automatic victory for either mind and no second simultaneously present exterior Rhaast.
03. Naafiri — DAGGER_PACK_MANIFESTATION. Established weapon category: dagger / living dune-hound pack. The Darkin spirit acts across a pack of dune hounds. Its existence involves multiple actual animals; do not depict an independent humanoid Naafiri and a separate pack by default.
04. Zaahen — GLAIVE_SOURCE_WALKING_FREE_BRANCH_HOST_UNVERIFIED. Established weapon category: glaive. The Unsundered willingly accepted weapon imprisonment to resist corruption. Riot’s contemporary biography describes him walking free, but the corresponding Xin Zhao sacrifice, vessel and timing are not automatically events in this divergent branch.
05. Xolaani — BLOODLETTERS_UNRELEASED_BRANCH. Established weapon category: bloodletters. Ancient hemomancer who seeks peace by enslaving free will; the LoR Darkin Saga Jun host, blood army, Mihira and apocalypse resolutions remain conditional/unselected.
06. Taarosh — HALBERD_BOUND_UNLESS_ENACTED. Established weapon category: halberd. Ancient opponent of Xolaani, linked to Shadow Isles/Blessed Isles weapon history; later LoR manifestation is not automatically complete.
07. Joraal — SHIELD_BOUND_UNLESS_ENACTED. Established weapon category: aegis / shield. Void War trauma and formerly powerful protective service remain fundamental; Demacian custody, host and awakening require actual access.
08. Horazi — LODESTONE_BOUND_UNLESS_ENACTED. Established weapon category: lodestone / celestial orb. Mount Targon/celestial connections and Darkin resentments; no omniscience or automatic LoR golem host.
09. Naganeka of Zuretta — BALLISTA_BOUND_UNLESS_ENACTED. Established weapon category: ballista. Survived the historical Twilight of the Gods gathering; the later Darkin-ballista account does not imply a second Naganeka or automatic present host.
10. Anaakca — SPEAR_BOUND_UNLESS_ENACTED. Established weapon category: spear. Freljord-associated weapon; the Winter’s Claw and a prospective bearer retain agency and regional knowledge conditions.
11. Ibaaros — HARPOON_BOUND_UNLESS_ENACTED. Established weapon category: harpoon. Serpent Isles/sea association; prospective sea-monster vessel and Xolaani subordination require their individual story-gated circumstances.
12. Baalkux — STAFF_BANDLEWOOD_CONDITIONAL. Established weapon category: staff. Bandlewood prison may affect plants; a LoR tree manifestation requires the actual magical circumstance, not flesh/blood mechanics invented for wood.
13. Praa — FAN_SPIRIT_REALM_CONDITIONAL. Established weapon category: fan. Spirit Realm-associated Darkin; entering physical Runeterra and any host/spirit access need distinct evidence and valid passage.
14. Styraatu — HARP_ZAUN_CONDITIONAL. Established weapon category: harp. Weapon hidden beneath Zaun and associated with Chirean access in LoR; discovery and possession are not retroactively enacted by the S2 Zaun storyline.
15. Cebotaru — ANCIENT_SURVIVOR_FATE_UNKNOWN. No reliably assigned modern weapon. The Wolf survived the destructive Twilight of the Gods gathering; later death, imprisonment, weapon, modern location and access are unestablished.
16. Valeeva — ANCIENT_SURVIVOR_FATE_UNKNOWN. No reliably assigned modern weapon. Ancient gathering survivor. The story mentions an unnamed brother; secondary creator-attributed material links a sister relationship to Varus without establishing its precise biological nature. Neither definite blood kinship nor definite non-kinship is certified here. No confirmed modern weapon, host or whereabouts, and no Varus deployment follows.
17. Ta’anari — ANCIENT_CONFIRMED_DEAD_HISTORICAL. No reliably assigned modern weapon. Historical arranger of Twilight of the Gods’ lethal meeting; divested of divine immortality and killed by Myisha. Do not resurrect.
18. Xuuyan — ANCIENT_CONFIRMED_DEAD_HISTORICAL. No reliably assigned modern weapon. Killed by Ta’anari during the ancient meeting. Historical only.
19. Syphax — ANCIENT_CONFIRMED_DEAD_HISTORICAL. No reliably assigned modern weapon. Destroyed by the Chalicar’s celestial blast in Twilight of the Gods.
20. Zigantus — ANCIENT_CONFIRMED_DEAD_HISTORICAL. No reliably assigned modern weapon. Destroyed by the celestial attack in Twilight of the Gods.
21. Shabaka — ANCIENT_CONFIRMED_DEAD_HISTORICAL. No reliably assigned modern weapon. One of the seer twins, destroyed in the ancient celestial attack.
22. Shabake — ANCIENT_CONFIRMED_DEAD_HISTORICAL. No reliably assigned modern weapon. The other seer twin, destroyed alongside Shabaka in the ancient celestial attack.
23. Enakai — ANCIENT_CONFIRMED_DEAD_HISTORICAL. No reliably assigned modern weapon. Destroyed by the celestial attack while attending the gathering, despite his prior respect for Ta’anari.

SEASON 3 COMEDY BRAINSTORM — SAVED PREMISES, NOT PLAYED EVENTS
Author-selected anchor: Billy's persistent envy and prehistoric glory-day disparagement of Jormungandr. Individual situations and sample lines below are optional original calibration, not delivered dialogue or predetermined scenes. Preserve all established speech layers and independent wants.
- Billy: disputed old grandeur. Example claim: "In my prime, I could've thrown that over a mountain." Follow-up questions expose changing definitions of prime, thrown and mountain.
